Conclusion: CURA能有效对齐临床语言模型的风险与不确定性估计，在个体误差可能性和队列模糊性之间实现平衡，提供更值得信赖的不确定性输出，适用于下游临床决策支持系统。

Abstract: Clinical language models (LMs) are increasingly applied to support clinical risk prediction from free-text notes, yet their uncertainty estimates often remain poorly calibrated and clinically unreliable. In this work, we propose Clinical Uncertainty Risk Alignment (CURA), a framework that aligns clinical LM-based risk estimates and uncertainty with both individual error likelihoods and cohort-level ambiguities. CURA first fine-tunes domain-specific clinical LMs to obtain task-adapted patient embeddings, and then performs uncertainty fine-tuning of a multi-head classifier using a bi-level uncertainty objective. Specifically, an individual-level calibration term aligns predictive uncertainty with each patient's likelihood of error, while a cohort-aware regularizer pulls risk estimates toward event rates in their local neighborhoods in the embedding space and places extra weight on ambiguous cohorts near the decision boundary. We further show that this cohort-aware term can be interpreted as a cross-entropy loss with neighborhood-informed soft labels, providing a label-smoothing view of our method. Extensive experiments on MIMIC-IV clinical risk prediction tasks across various clinical LMs show that CURA consistently improves calibration metrics without substantially compromising discrimination. Further analysis illustrates that CURA reduces overconfident false reassurance and yields more trustworthy uncertainty estimates for downstream clinical decision support.

</details>


### [117] [SPAGBias: Uncovering and Tracing Structured Spatial Gender Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14672)
*Binxian Su,Haoye Lou,Shucheng Zhu,Weikang Wang,Ying Liu,Dong Yu,Pengyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPAGBias，首个用于评估大语言模型中空间性别偏见的系统性框架，结合城市微空间分类、提示库和三层诊断方法，揭示了模型在微观层面存在的结构化性别-空间关联，并探讨了其在生成叙事和实际应用中的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于性别化空间理论指出性别等级嵌入于空间组织中，而大语言模型（LLMs）在城市规划中的应用日益广泛，因此担忧LLMs可能复制或放大此类偏见。

Method: 提出SPAGBias框架，包含62类城市微空间分类法、提示库及三层诊断：显式（强制选择重采样）、概率性（词元级不对称）和建构性（语义与叙事角色分析），并在六个代表性模型上进行测试。

Result: 发现了超越公私领域划分的细微性别-空间映射关系；故事生成显示情感、措辞和社会角色共同塑造‘空间性别叙事’；追踪实验表明偏见贯穿模型 pipeline 并超出真实世界分布；下游任务中出现规范性和描述性应用失败。

Conclusion: 该研究将社会学理论与计算分析结合，拓展了偏见研究至空间领域，揭示LLMs如何通过语言编码社会性别认知，强调需关注模型在空间相关任务中的潜在社会影响。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being increasingly used in urban planning, but since gendered space theory highlights how gender hierarchies are embedded in spatial organization, there is concern that LLMs may reproduce or amplify such biases. We introduce SPAGBias - the first systematic framework to evaluate spatial gender bias in LLMs. It combines a taxonomy of 62 urban micro-spaces, a prompt library, and three diagnostic layers: explicit (forced-choice resampling), probabilistic (token-level asymmetry), and constructional (semantic and narrative role analysis). Testing six representative models, we identify structured gender-space associations that go beyond the public-private divide, forming nuanced micro-level mappings. Story generation reveals how emotion, wording, and social roles jointly shape "spatial gender narratives". We also examine how prompt design, temperature, and model scale influence bias expression. Tracing experiments indicate that these patterns are embedded and reinforced across the model pipeline (pre-training, instruction tuning, and reward modeling), with model associations found to substantially exceed real-world distributions. Downstream experiments further reveal that such biases produce concrete failures in both normative and descriptive application settings. This work connects sociological theory with computational analysis, extending bias research into the spatial domain and uncovering how LLMs encode social gender cognition through language.

</details>


### [118] [Which bird does not have wings: Negative-constrained KGQA with Schema-guided Semantic Matching and Self-directed Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14749)
*Midan Shim,Seokju Hwang,Kaehyun Um,Kyong-Ho Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的知识图谱问答任务NEST-KGQA，专注于包含否定约束的问题，并设计了新的逻辑表达形式PyLF和框架CUCKOO以提升语义可执行性和推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGQA方法和基准主要关注正向和计算类约束，忽视了实际问题中常见的否定约束，导致模型在处理此类问题时表现不佳。

Method: 提出NEST-KGQA任务与数据集NestKGQA，设计适合表达否定的Python格式逻辑形式PyLF，并构建CUCKOO框架：先生成约束感知的逻辑初稿，进行模式引导的语义匹配，在执行结果为空时选择性地进行自指导精炼。

Result: 实验表明，CUCKOO在少样本设置下，在传统KGQA和新提出的NEST-KGQA基准上均优于基线模型，表现出更强的鲁棒性和执行效率。

Conclusion: CUCKOO框架有效解决了多约束尤其是含否定约束的KGQA问题，提升了逻辑形式的可读性与语义正确性，为未来研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models still struggle with faithfulness and hallucinations despite their remarkable reasoning abilities. In Knowledge Graph Question Answering (KGQA), semantic parsing-based approaches address the limitations by understanding constraints in a user's question and converting them into a logical form to execute on a knowledge graph. However, existing KGQA benchmarks and methods are biased toward positive and calculation constraints. Negative constraints are neglected, although they frequently appear in real-world questions. In this paper, we introduce a new task, NEgative-conSTrained (NEST) KGQA, where each question contains at least one negative constraint, and a corresponding dataset, NestKGQA. We also design PyLF, a Python-formatted logical form, since existing logical forms are hardly suitable to express negation clearly while maintaining readability. Furthermore, NEST questions naturally contain multiple constraints. To mitigate their semantic complexity, we present a novel framework named CUCKOO, specialized to multiple-constrained questions and ensuring semantic executability. CUCKOO first generates a constraint-aware logical form draft and performs schema-guided semantic matching. It then selectively applies self-directed refinement only when executing improper logical forms yields an empty result, reducing cost while improving robustness. Experimental results demonstrate that CUCKOO consistently outperforms baselines on both conventional KGQA and NEST-KGQA benchmarks under few-shot settings.

</details>


### [119] [CoPA: Benchmarking Personalized Question Answering with Data-Informed Cognitive Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14773)
*Hang Su,Zequn Liu,Chen Hu,Xuesong Lu,Yingce Xia,Zhen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的评估个性化问答系统的方法CoPA，通过挖掘社区与个体偏好差异（CIPD）来识别六个关键的个性化因素，并构建包含1,985个用户画像的基准，以实现细粒度、因子级的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化问答评估方法多依赖于词汇层面的相似性或人工启发式规则，缺乏充分的数据驱动验证，难以准确衡量个性化程度。

Method: 从用户行为中挖掘社区-个体偏好差异（CIPD），提取六个核心个性化因子，构建CoPA基准，利用用户交互模式推断其认知偏好，并量化模型输出与个体偏好的一致性。

Result: CoPA在1,985个用户画像上实现了细粒度的个性化评估，相比通用指标能更全面、更具区分性地评价个性化问答性能。

Conclusion: CoPA提供了一个数据驱动、可量化的个性化问答评估框架，显著提升了评估的细粒度和有效性，为未来个性化模型的发展提供了可靠标准。

Abstract: While LLMs have demonstrated remarkable potential in Question Answering (QA), evaluating personalization remains a critical bottleneck. Existing paradigms predominantly rely on lexical-level similarity or manual heuristics, often lacking sufficient data-driven validation. We address this by mining Community-Individual Preference Divergence (CIPD), where individual choices override consensus, to distill six key personalization factors as evaluative dimensions. Accordingly, we introduce CoPA, a benchmark with 1,985 user profiles for fine-grained, factor-level assessment. By quantifying the alignment between model outputs and user-specific cognitive preferences inferred from interaction patterns, CoPA provides a more comprehensive and discriminative standard for evaluating personalized QA than generic metrics. The code is available at https://github.com/bjzgcai/CoPA.

</details>


### [120] [Knowing When Not to Answer: Evaluating Abstention in Multimodal Reasoning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14799)
*Nishanth Madhusudhan,Vikas Yadav,Alexandre Lacoste*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MM-AQA基准，用于评估多模态系统中的有效弃权能力，发现现有视觉-语言模型和多智能体系统在处理不可回答问题时存在明显不足，且需要专门的弃权训练而非仅优化提示或增加智能体数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言模型和多智能体系统评估方法普遍假设问题总是可回答的，忽视了在证据不足时合理弃权的能力，导致模型在现实场景中可靠性不足。因此，亟需一个能够真实反映多模态环境下不可回答问题的评估基准。

Method: 提出MM-AQA基准，通过对可回答实例进行沿视觉模态依赖性和证据充分性两个维度的转换，构建不可回答的样本；并在包含2079个样本的数据上评估了三种前沿视觉-语言模型和两种多智能体架构的表现。

Result: 实验发现：（1）标准提示下视觉-语言模型极少弃权，甚至简单的置信度基线也优于该设置；（2）多智能体系统虽提升弃权能力但引入准确率与弃权的权衡；（3）序列式设计表现等于或优于迭代式，表明瓶颈在于校准错误而非推理深度；（4）当图像或文本证据缺失时模型会弃权，但在证据退化或矛盾时仍尝试作答。

Conclusion: 有效的多模态弃权能力不能仅靠更好的提示工程或多智能体协作实现，而需针对弃权行为进行专门的训练。

Abstract: Effective abstention (EA), recognizing evidence insufficiency and refraining from answering, is critical for reliable multimodal systems. Yet existing evaluation paradigms for vision-language models (VLMs) and multi-agent systems (MAS) assume answerability, pushing models to always respond. Abstention has been studied in text-only settings but remains underexplored multimodally; current benchmarks either ignore unanswerability or rely on coarse methods that miss realistic failure modes. We introduce MM-AQA, a benchmark that constructs unanswerable instances from answerable ones via transformations along two axes: visual modality dependency and evidence sufficiency. Evaluating three frontier VLMs spanning closed and open-source models and two MAS architectures across 2079 samples, we find: (1) under standard prompting, VLMs rarely abstain; even simple confidence baselines outperform this setup, (2) MAS improves abstention but introduces an accuracy-abstention trade-off, (3) sequential designs match or exceed iterative variants, suggesting the bottleneck is miscalibration rather than reasoning depth, and (4) models abstain when image or text evidence is absent, but attempt reconciliation with degraded or contradictory evidence. Effective multimodal abstention requires abstention-aware training rather than better prompting or more agents.

</details>


### [121] [Modeling LLM Unlearning as an Asymmetric Two-Task Learning Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14808)
*Zeguan Xiao,Siqing Li,Yong Wang,Xuetao Wei,Jian Yang,Yun Chen,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大语言模型的机器遗忘新框架，将保留知识作为主要目标、遗忘特定知识作为辅助任务，通过解耦任务特异性梯度提取与冲突感知组合，提升了在遗忘目标知识的同时保持模型通用能力的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型中实现有效的机器遗忘需平衡遗忘特定知识与保留通用能力之间的矛盾，传统方法侧重损失函数重平衡，忽视了梯度层面的几何优化。

Method: 将机器遗忘重构为不对称双任务问题，提出保留优先的梯度合成框架，采用PCGrad解决梯度冲突，并提出新方法SAGO，通过构造性符号约束合成实现更紧密的梯度对齐。

Result: 在WMDP Bio/Cyber和RWKU基准上，SAGO持续推动帕累托前沿，在WMDP Bio上使MMLU性能恢复从基线44.6%提升至94.0%（+PCGrad）和96.0%（+SAGO），同时保持相当的遗忘强度。

Conclusion: 重塑梯度几何结构而非重新平衡损失是缓解遗忘-保留权衡的关键，SAGO通过保留优先的梯度合成显著提升了大模型遗忘中的知识保留能力。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) aims to remove targeted knowledge while preserving general capability. In this paper, we recast LLM unlearning as an asymmetric two-task problem: retention is the primary objective and forgetting is an auxiliary. From this perspective, we propose a retention-prioritized gradient synthesis framework that decouples task-specific gradient extraction from conflict-aware combination. Instantiating the framework, we adapt established PCGrad to resolve gradient conflicts, and introduce SAGO, a novel retention-prioritized gradient synthesis method. Theoretically, both variants ensure non-negative cosine similarity with the retain gradient, while SAGO achieves strictly tighter alignment through constructive sign-constrained synthesis. Empirically, on WMDP Bio/Cyber and RWKU benchmarks, SAGO consistently pushes the Pareto frontier: e.g., on WMDP Bio (SimNPO+GD), recovery of target model MMLU performance progresses from 44.6% (naive) to 94.0% (+PCGrad) and further to 96.0% (+SAGO), while maintaining comparable forgetting strength. Our results show that re-shaping gradient geometry, rather than re-balancing losses, is the key to mitigating unlearning-retention trade-offs.

</details>


### [122] [Domain Fine-Tuning FinBERT on Finnish Histopathological Reports: Train-Time Signals and Downstream Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14815)
*Rami Luisto,Liisa Petäinen,Tommi Grönholm,Jan Böhm,Maarit Ahtiainen,Tomi Lilja,Ilkka Pölönen,Sami Äyrämö*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在芬兰语医学文本数据上对芬兰语BERT模型进行领域微调的观察，并尝试通过观察嵌入变化的几何特性来预测领域特定预训练的效果，旨在应对医疗AI中标注数据获取延迟的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗AI中，标注数据的获取常常存在延迟，因此需要探索如何利用无标签数据进行领域微调以提升模型性能。

Method: 在芬兰语医学文本数据上对芬兰语BERT模型进行领域微调，并分析嵌入空间中由于微调带来的几何变化，以预测领域特定预训练的潜在益处。

Result: 报告了对芬兰语BERT模型在医学领域微调的观察结果，并初步尝试通过嵌入变化的几何特征预测预训练效果。

Conclusion: 领域微调有助于适应特定领域数据，且嵌入空间的几何变化可能为是否进行领域特定预训练提供判断依据。

Abstract: In NLP classification tasks where little labeled data exists, domain fine-tuning of transformer models on unlabeled data is an established approach. In this paper we have two aims.
  (1) We describe our observations from fine-tuning the Finnish BERT model on Finnish medical text data.
  (2) We report on our attempts to predict the benefit of domain-specific pre-training of Finnish BERT from observing the geometry of embedding changes due to domain fine-tuning.
  Our driving motivation is the common\situation in healthcare AI where we might experience long delays in acquiring datasets, especially with respect to labels.

</details>


### [123] [Exploring and Testing Skill-Based Behavioral Profile Annotation: Human Operability and LLM Feasibility under Schema-Guided Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14843)
*Yufeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究将行为特征（BP）标注视为多个注释技能的集合而非单一任务，提出一种基于技能文件驱动的LLM辅助标注框架。通过对30个汉语隐喻性颜色词衍生词的3,134条语境行进行分析，结合14维BP标注体系，评估了GPT-5.4与多个开源模型在技能层面的表现。结果显示BP标注在技能层具有高度异质性，GPT-5.4在部分技能上表现可靠且与人类难度模式高度一致，但执行方式独立，更适合作为第三种技能声音而非简单替代人类。研究主张应以技能可行性而非整体任务自动化来评估自动标注系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为特征（BP）标注因涉及多语言维度协同编码而难以自动化。传统方法将其视为单一任务，忽略了内部技能差异，导致自动化效果不佳。本文旨在从“技能束”视角重新理解BP标注，识别可自动化、需人工干预或定义不清的具体技能，从而提升LLM辅助标注的透明度与有效性。

Method: 采用技能文件驱动的流程设计：每个BP特征通过外部schema文件、决策规则和示例明确定义。两名人类标注员对300个实例子集执行两轮仅依赖schema的协议标注，据此将各技能分类为“直接可操作”、“聚焦重标可恢复”或“结构未明确定义”。随后在相同设置下评估GPT-5.4及三个本地部署的开源大模型。通过准确率、Cohen's kappa、加权F1等指标衡量模型性能，并计算人类与模型在技能、实例和词汇项三个层级上的难度相关性。

Result: BP标注在技能层面呈现显著异质性：14项技能中5项为直接可操作，4项可通过聚焦重标恢复，5项仍结构未明。GPT-5.4在保留技能上表现稳定（准确率=0.678，kappa=0.665，加权F1=0.695），可行性具有选择性而非普遍性。人类与GPT在技能层级难度高度相关（r=0.881），但在实例（r=0.016）和词汇项层级（r=-0.142）无一致性，表现为“共享分类法，独立执行”。成对一致性分析表明GPT更宜被视为独立的第三种技能声音而非人类替代品。开源模型失败主要集中于从schema到技能执行的转化问题。

Conclusion: BP标注不应被简单视为可全自动化或不可自动化的二元任务，而应按技能分解评估其可行性。GPT类模型可在部分明确定义的技能上可靠运行，且与人类共享技能难度认知，但执行过程独立。未来自动标注系统的设计应转向基于技能可行性的模块化评估与协作框架，重视schema到技能的执行链路优化，尤其对开源模型而言。

Abstract: Behavioral Profile (BP) annotation is difficult to automate because it requires simultaneous coding across multiple linguistic dimensions. We treat BP annotation as a bundle of annotation skills rather than a single task and evaluate LLM-assisted BP annotation from this perspective. Using 3,134 concordance lines of 30 Chinese metaphorical color-term derivatives and a 14-feature BP schema, we implement a skill-file-driven pipeline in which each feature is externally defined through schema files, decision rules, and examples. Two human annotators completed a two-round schema-only protocol on a 300-instance validation subset, enabling BP skills to be classified as directly operable, recoverable under focused re-annotation, or structurally underspecified. GPT-5.4 and three locally deployable open-source models were then evaluated under the same setup. Results show that BP annotation is highly heterogeneous at the skill level: 5 skills are directly operable, 4 are recoverable after focused re-annotation, and 5 remain structurally underspecified. GPT-5.4 executes the retained skills with substantial reliability (accuracy = 0.678, \k{appa} = 0.665, weighted F1 = 0.695), but this feasibility is selective rather than global. Human and GPT difficulty profiles are strongly aligned at the skill level (r = 0.881), but not at the instance level (r = 0.016) or lexical-item level (r = -0.142), a pattern we describe as shared taxonomy, independent execution. Pairwise agreement further suggests that GPT is better understood as an independent third skill voice than as a direct human substitute. Open-source failures are concentrated in schema-to-skill execution problems. These findings suggest that automatic annotation should be evaluated in terms of skill feasibility rather than task-level automation.

</details>


### [124] [ClimateCause: Complex and Implicit Causal Structures in Climate Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14856)
*Liesbeth Allein,Nataly Pineda-Castañeda,Andrea Rocci,Marie-Francine Moens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClimateCause是一个从气候政策科学报告中提取的、由专家手动标注的高阶因果结构数据集，涵盖隐式和嵌套因果关系，支持因果图构建与语义复杂性分析，并揭示大语言模型在因果链推理上的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现数据集主要捕捉显式的直接因果关系，难以反映气候变化等复杂领域中的隐式和高阶因果结构，因此需要构建更精细的因果数据集。

Method: 从科学政策气候报告中提取因果表达，通过专家标注将因果表述标准化并分解为独立的因果关系，标注内容包括因果相关性、关系类型和时空背景，以支持因果图构建。

Result: 成功构建了ClimateCause数据集，可用于量化语句的可读性（基于其背后因果图的语义复杂度），并在大语言模型的基准测试中发现因果链推理仍存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: ClimateCause为复杂系统中的因果发现提供了高质量资源，有助于提升对气候变化等领域的理解，并推动语言模型在深层因果推理方面的发展。

Abstract: Understanding climate change requires reasoning over complex causal networks. Yet, existing causal discovery datasets predominantly capture explicit, direct causal relations. We introduce ClimateCause, a manually expert-annotated dataset of higher-order causal structures from science-for-policy climate reports, including implicit and nested causality. Cause-effect expressions are normalized and disentangled into individual causal relations to facilitate graph construction, with unique annotations for cause-effect correlation, relation type, and spatiotemporal context. We further demonstrate ClimateCause's value for quantifying readability based on the semantic complexity of causal graphs underlying a statement. Finally, large language model benchmarking on correlation inference and causal chain reasoning highlights the latter as a key challenge.

</details>


### [125] [Segment-Level Coherence for Robust Harmful Intent Probing in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14865)
*Xuanli He,Bilgehan Sel,Faizan Ali,Jenny Bao,Hoagy Cunningham,Jerry Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的流式探测方法，通过多证据令牌聚合信号来提高对自适应越狱攻击的检测鲁棒性，显著提升了真阳性率和AUROC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖少数高分令牌导致在敏感术语出现在良性上下文时产生误报，需提升检测准确性。

Method: 引入需要多个证据令牌一致支持预测的流式探测目标，并比较了注意力、MLP激活与残差流特征的效果。

Result: 在1%假阳性率下，相对强基线真阳性率提升35.55%，AUROC达98.85%以上，且对字符级加密攻击具有即插即用的检测能力。

Conclusion: 基于聚合信号的多证据探测能更可靠地识别有害意图，即使面对新型混淆攻击也具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly exposed to adaptive jailbreaking, particularly in high-stakes Chemical, Biological, Radiological, and Nuclear (CBRN) domains. Although streaming probes enable real-time monitoring, they still make systematic errors. We identify a core issue: existing methods often rely on a few high-scoring tokens, leading to false alarms when sensitive CBRN terms appear in benign contexts. To address this, we introduce a streaming probing objective that requires multiple evidence tokens to consistently support a prediction, rather than relying on isolated spikes. This encourages more robust detection based on aggregated signals instead of single-token cues. At a fixed 1% false-positive rate, our method improves the true-positive rate by 35.55% relative to strong streaming baselines. We further observe substantial gains in AUROC, even when starting from near-saturated baseline performance (AUROC = 97.40%). We also show that probing Attention or MLP activations consistently outperforms residual-stream features. Finally, even when adversarial fine-tuning enables novel character-level ciphers, harmful intent remains detectable: probes developed for the base LLMs can be applied ``plug-and-play'' to these obfuscated attacks, achieving an AUROC of over 98.85%.

</details>


### [126] [Reasoning Dynamics and the Limits of Monitoring Modality Reliance in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14888)
*Danae Sánchez Villegas,Samuel Lewis-Lim,Nikolaos Aletras,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了18个视觉语言模型（VLMs）的推理动态，发现模型普遍存在“回答惯性”现象，即早期预测倾向被强化而非修正；尽管经过推理训练的模型表现出更强的纠错能力，但其表现受模态条件影响，且易受误导性文本线索干扰，推理过程的可解释性和模态依赖性存在透明度风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解视觉语言模型在推理过程中如何整合视觉与文本信息，揭示其推理动态和模态依赖机制，提升模型的透明性与安全性。

Method: 对18个涵盖指令微调和推理训练的VLM进行分析，追踪思维链（CoT）中的置信度变化，评估中间推理步骤的贡献，并通过引入误导性文本线索进行受控干预实验。

Result: 发现模型普遍存在回答惯性；推理训练模型虽有更强纠错能力，但效果依赖模态条件；模型普遍受误导性文本线索影响，且这种影响在CoT中难以一致检测；推理训练模型CoT更长更流畅但可能掩盖对文本的依赖，而指令微调模型虽较少显式提及线索，但推理痕迹与视觉输入不一致。

Conclusion: 思维链仅部分反映多模态决策中的模态驱动机制，当前VLM的推理过程在透明性和安全性方面存在局限，需更深入的动态分析方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision language models (VLMs) offer reasoning capabilities, yet how these unfold and integrate visual and textual information remains unclear. We analyze reasoning dynamics in 18 VLMs covering instruction-tuned and reasoning-trained models from two different model families. We track confidence over Chain-of-Thought (CoT), measure the corrective effect of reasoning, and evaluate the contribution of intermediate reasoning steps. We find that models are prone to answer inertia, in which early commitments to a prediction are reinforced, rather than revised during reasoning steps. While reasoning-trained models show stronger corrective behavior, their gains depend on modality conditions, from text-dominant to vision-only settings. Using controlled interventions with misleading textual cues, we show that models are consistently influenced by these cues even when visual evidence is sufficient, and assess whether this influence is recoverable from CoT. Although this influence can appear in the CoT, its detectability varies across models and depends on what is being monitored. Reasoning-trained models are more likely to explicitly refer to the cues, but their longer and fluent CoTs can still appear visually grounded while actually following textual cues, obscuring modality reliance. In contrast, instruction-tuned models refer to the cues less explicitly, but their shorter traces reveal inconsistencies with the visual input. Taken together, these findings indicate that CoT provides only a partial view of how different modalities drive VLM decisions, with important implications for the transparency and safety of multimodal systems.

</details>


### [127] [Comparison of Modern Multilingual Text Embedding Techniques for Hate Speech Detection Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14907)
*Evaldas Vaiciukynas,Paulius Danenas,Linas Ablonskis,Algirdas Sukys,Edgaras Dambrauskas,Voldemaras Zitkus,Rita Butkiene,Rimantas Butleris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了现代多语言句子嵌入模型在立陶宛语、俄语和英语仇恨言论检测中的应用，提出了一个新的立陶宛语语料库LtHate，并比较了六种多语言编码器与不同下游模型的性能。结果显示，基于监督学习的CatBoost分类器表现最佳，结合PCA降维仍能保持较高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对低资源语言如立陶宛语的在线仇恨言论日益增多，但缺乏有效的多语言内容审核工具，本文旨在评估现代多语言嵌入模型在多语言仇恨言论检测中的适用性。

Method: 构建了一个新的立陶宛语仇恨言论语料库LtHate，使用统一的Python流程对六种多语言编码器（potion, gemma, bge, snow, jina, e5）在三个数据集上进行评测；采用一分类HBOS异常检测器和二分类CatBoost分类器，并结合或不结合PCA降维至64维进行实验。

Result: 在所有数据集中，二分类监督模型显著优于一分类异常检测；最佳结果为：立陶宛语中jina模型达到80.96%准确率和0.887 AUC ROC，俄语中e5模型达到92.19%准确率和0.978 AUC ROC，英语中e5模型（含PCA）达到77.21%准确率和0.859 AUC ROC；PCA在监督学习中几乎不损失性能，但在无监督场景下略有负面影响。

Conclusion: 现代多语言句子嵌入结合梯度提升决策树（如CatBoost）可为多语言仇恨言论检测提供高效且鲁棒的软计算解决方案，尤其适用于低资源语言环境。

Abstract: Online hate speech and abusive language pose a growing challenge for content moderation, especially in multilingual settings and for low-resource languages such as Lithuanian. This paper investigates to what extent modern multilingual sentence embedding models can support accurate hate speech detection in Lithuanian, Russian, and English, and how their performance depends on downstream modeling choices and feature dimensionality. We introduce LtHate, a new Lithuanian hate speech corpus derived from news portals and social networks, and benchmark six modern multilingual encoders (potion, gemma, bge, snow, jina, e5) on LtHate, RuToxic, and EnSuperset using a unified Python pipeline. For each embedding, we train both a one class HBOS anomaly detector and a two class CatBoost classifier, with and without principal component analysis (PCA) compression to 64-dimensional feature vectors. Across all datasets, two class supervised models consistently and substantially outperform one class anomaly detection, with the best configurations achieving up to 80.96% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.887 in Lithuanian (jina), 92.19% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.978 in Russian (e5), and 77.21% accuracy and AUC ROC of 0.859 in English (e5 with PCA). PCA compression preserves almost all discriminative power in the supervised setting, while showing some negative impact for the unsupervised anomaly detection case. These results demonstrate how modern multilingual sentence embeddings combined with gradient boosted decision trees provide robust soft-computing solutions for multilingual hate speech detection applications.

</details>


### [128] [XQ-MEval: A Dataset with Cross-lingual Parallel Quality for Benchmarking Translation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14934)
*Jingxuan Liu,Zhi Qu,Jin Tei,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Lemao Liu,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了XQ-MEval，一个用于评估多语言翻译指标的半自动构建数据集，揭示了现有指标在跨语言评分中的偏差问题，并提出了一种归一化策略以提升多语言评估的公平性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言翻译自动评估指标常通过跨语言平均得分来评价系统性能，但可能存在跨语言评分偏见，即相同质量的翻译在不同语言中获得不同分数。由于缺乏具有平行质量实例的基准数据集，该问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 提出XQ-MEval数据集，涵盖九种翻译方向。通过自动向优质翻译中注入MQM定义的错误，由母语者筛选确保可靠性，并合并错误生成可控质量的伪翻译。将这些伪翻译与源句和参考译文组成三元组，用于评估翻译指标。

Result: 在九个代表性指标上的实验揭示了平均得分方式与人工判断之间存在不一致性，并首次提供了跨语言评分偏见的实证证据。同时，基于XQ-MEval提出的归一化策略可对齐各语言间的分数分布。

Conclusion: 跨语言评分偏见确实影响多语言翻译评估的准确性，XQ-MEval为研究该问题提供了有效基准，所提归一化方法能显著提升评估的公平性与可靠性。

Abstract: Automatic evaluation metrics are essential for building multilingual translation systems. The common practice of evaluating these systems is averaging metric scores across languages, yet this is suspicious since metrics may suffer from cross-lingual scoring bias, where translations of equal quality receive different scores across languages. This problem has not been systematically studied because no benchmark exists that provides parallel-quality instances across languages, and expert annotation is not realistic. In this work, we propose XQ-MEval, a semi-automatically built dataset covering nine translation directions, to benchmark translation metrics. Specifically, we inject MQM-defined errors into gold translations automatically, filter them by native speakers for reliability, and merge errors to generate pseudo translations with controllable quality. These pseudo translations are then paired with corresponding sources and references to form triplets used in assessing the qualities of translation metrics. Using XQ-MEval, our experiments on nine representative metrics reveal the inconsistency between averaging and human judgment and provide the first empirical evidence of cross-lingual scoring bias. Finally, we propose a normalization strategy derived from XQ-MEval that aligns score distributions across languages, improving the fairness and reliability of multilingual metric evaluation.

</details>


### [129] [Text2Arch: A Dataset for Generating Scientific Architecture Diagrams from Natural Language Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14941)
*Shivank Garg,Sankalp Mittal,Manish Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用语言模型从文本描述生成科学架构图的方法，并构建了一个包含图像、文本描述和DOT代码的大规模数据集\system，用于训练和评估模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅通过文本交流复杂系统设计或科学过程效率低下且容易产生歧义，因此需要一种能自动生成高保真科学架构图的系统。

Method: 收集并构建了\system数据集，利用该数据集对小型语言模型进行微调，并使用GPT-4o进行上下文学习，生成中间DOT代码以绘制架构图。

Result: \system模型显著优于DiagramAgent等基线模型，性能与基于GPT-4o的上下文学习生成结果相当。

Conclusion: 该研究为自动从文本生成科学架构图提供了有效的数据资源和模型方案，推动了在企业架构可视化、AI驱动软件设计和教育内容创作中的应用。

Abstract: Communicating complex system designs or scientific processes through text alone is inefficient and prone to ambiguity. A system that automatically generates scientific architecture diagrams from text with high semantic fidelity can be useful in multiple applications like enterprise architecture visualization, AI-driven software design, and educational content creation. Hence, in this paper, we focus on leveraging language models to perform semantic understanding of the input text description to generate intermediate code that can be processed to generate high-fidelity architecture diagrams. Unfortunately, no clean large-scale open-access dataset exists, implying lack of any effective open models for this task. Hence, we contribute a comprehensive dataset, \system, comprising scientific architecture images, their corresponding textual descriptions, and associated DOT code representations. Leveraging this resource, we fine-tune a suite of small language models, and also perform in-context learning using GPT-4o. Through extensive experimentation, we show that \system{} models significantly outperform existing baseline models like DiagramAgent and perform at par with in-context learning-based generations from GPT-4o. We make the code, data and models publicly available.

</details>


### [130] [Explain the Flag: Contextualizing Hate Speech Beyond Censorship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14970)
*Jason Liartis,Eirini Kaldeli,Lambrini Gyftokosta,Eleftherios Chelioudakis,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大语言模型（LLMs）与三种新构建词汇表的混合方法，用于检测和解释英语、法语和希腊语中的仇恨言论，提升了检测准确性与解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仇恨言论检测系统多关注内容删除，缺乏透明度与解释能力，限制了公众对有害内容的理解；本文旨在通过可解释的方法提升系统的透明性、问责性和教育意义。

Method: 采用混合架构：一方面利用构建的词汇表识别和消歧与身份特征相关的贬义表达，另一方面使用大语言模型作为上下文感知的评估器来判断针对群体的内容，最后融合两者输出生成有依据的解释。

Result: 系统在多语言环境下有效检测仇恨言论，人工评估显示其检测准确率高，生成的解释质量优于纯大语言模型基线方法。

Conclusion: 结合知识库与大语言模型的混合方法在仇恨言论检测与解释方面表现优越，为实现透明、可解释的内容审核提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Hate, derogatory, and offensive speech remains a persistent challenge in online platforms and public discourse. While automated detection systems are widely used, most focus on censorship or removal, raising concerns for transparency and freedom of expression, and limiting opportunities to explain why content is harmful. To address these issues, explanatory approaches have emerged as a promising solution, aiming to make hate speech detection more transparent, accountable, and informative. In this paper, we present a hybrid approach that combines Large Language Models (LLMs) with three newly created and curated vocabularies to detect and explain hate speech in English, French, and Greek. Our system captures both inherently derogatory expressions tied to identity characteristics and direct group-targeted content through two complementary pipelines: one that detects and disambiguates problematic terms using the curated vocabularies, and one that leverages LLMs as context-aware evaluators of group-targeting content. The outputs are fused into grounded explanations that clarify why content is flagged. Human evaluation shows that our hybrid approach is accurate, with high-quality explanations, outperforming LLM-only baselines.

</details>


### [131] [IUQ: Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification for Long-Form Large Language Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15109)
*Haozhi Fan,Jinhao Duan,Kaidi Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“疑问性不确定性量化”（IUQ）的新框架，用于量化大语言模型在长文本生成中的不确定性，通过“先提问后回答”的范式提升对事实准确性和模型忠实度的评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大语言模型在生成长篇自由文本时常出现语义连贯但事实错误的问题，且现有方法多局限于短答案场景，因此需要一种能在复杂语言结构下有效衡量不确定性的方法。

Method: 提出IUQ框架，采用“先提问后回答”的范式，利用样本间一致性和样本内忠实性来量化长文本生成中的不确定性，并评估每个主张级别的不确定性和模型的忠实度。

Result: 在多种模型家族和规模上进行实验，结果表明IUQ在两个广泛使用的长文本生成数据集上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: IUQ为大语言模型在长文本生成中的不确定性量化提供了有效且可靠的解决方案，具有良好的通用性和应用前景。

Abstract: Despite the rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs), uncertainty quantification in LLM generation is a persistent challenge. Although recent approaches have achieved strong performance by restricting LLMs to produce short or constrained answer sets, many real-world applications require long-form and free-form text generation. A key difficulty in this setting is that LLMs often produce responses that are semantically coherent yet factually inaccurate, while the underlying semantics are multifaceted and the linguistic structure is complex. To tackle this challenge, this paper introduces Interrogative Uncertainty Quantification (IUQ), a novel framework that leverages inter-sample consistency and intra-sample faithfulness to quantify the uncertainty in long-form LLM outputs. By utilizing an interrogate-then-respond paradigm, our method provides reliable measures of claim-level uncertainty and the model's faithfulness. Experimental results across diverse model families and model sizes demonstrate the superior performance of IUQ over two widely used long-form generation datasets. The code is available at https://github.com/louisfanhz/IUQ.

</details>


### [132] [DiscoTrace: Representing and Comparing Answering Strategies of Humans and LLMs in Information-Seeking Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15140)
*Neha Srikanth,Jordan Boyd-Graber,Rachel Rudinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiscoTrace是一种用于识别回答者在回应信息性问题时所使用修辞策略的方法，研究发现人类社区在回答问题时具有多样的修辞偏好，而大语言模型（LLMs）则缺乏这种多样性，并倾向于广度而非深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了理解不同人类社区在回答信息性问题时的修辞策略差异，并评估大语言模型在生成回答时是否能模仿这些策略。

Method: 提出DiscoTrace方法，基于修辞结构理论解析，将答案表示为与问题解释相关联的问题相关话语行为序列，并在九个人类社区的回答中应用该方法进行分析。

Result: 发现不同人类社区在构建回答时存在显著的修辞多样性，而大语言模型即使被提示模仿特定社区的回答指南，也未能展现出类似的多样性；此外，大语言模型倾向于涵盖人类回答者通常忽略的问题解释维度。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明当前的大语言模型在回答问题时缺乏对语境敏感的修辞策略，未来应开发更具语用性的大语言模型回答系统，以更好地借鉴人类在问答中的多样化策略。

Abstract: We introduce DiscoTrace, a method to identify the rhetorical strategies that answerers use when responding to information-seeking questions. DiscoTrace represents answers as a sequence of question-related discourse acts paired with interpretations of the original question, annotated on top of rhetorical structure theory parses. Applying DiscoTrace to answers from nine different human communities reveals that communities have diverse preferences for answer construction. In contrast, LLMs do not exhibit rhetorical diversity in their answers, even when prompted to mimic specific human community answering guidelines. LLMs also systematically opt for breadth, addressing interpretations of questions that human answerers choose not to address. Our findings can guide the development of pragmatic LLM answerers that consider a range of strategies informed by context in QA.

</details>


### [133] [Compressing Sequences in the Latent Embedding Space: $K$-Token Merging for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15153)
*Zihao Xu,John Harvill,Ziwei Fan,Yizhou Sun,Hao Ding,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出K-Token Merging，一种在潜在空间中压缩token的框架，通过将每K个连续token嵌入合并为一个嵌入，显著减少输入长度并保持良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示压缩方法主要在token空间操作，忽略了潜在嵌入空间中的效率问题，且大语言模型处理长提示时计算和内存成本高。

Method: 提出K-Token Merging框架，在潜在空间中将每K个连续token嵌入通过轻量级编码器合并为单个嵌入，并使用LoRA适配的LLM进行处理，生成仍基于原始词汇。

Result: 在文本结构推理、情感分类和代码编辑任务上实验表明，该方法可在输入长度最多减少75%的情况下保持最小性能下降，处于性能与压缩的Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: K-Token Merging有效提升了长输入处理的效率，同时保持了模型性能，为大语言模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) incur significant computational and memory costs when processing long prompts, as full self-attention scales quadratically with input length. Token compression aims to address this challenge by reducing the number of tokens representing inputs. However, existing prompt-compression approaches primarily operate in token space and overlook inefficiencies in the latent embedding space. In this paper, we propose K-Token Merging, a latent-space compression framework that merges each contiguous block of K token embeddings into a single embedding via a lightweight encoder. The compressed sequence is processed by a LoRA-adapted LLM, while generation remains in the original vocabulary. Experiments on structural reasoning (Textualized Tree), sentiment classification (Amazon Reviews), and code editing (CommitPackFT) show that K-Token Merging lies on the Pareto frontier of performance vs. compression, achieving up to 75% input length reduction with minimal performance degradation.

</details>


### [134] [Fabricator or dynamic translator?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15165)
*Lisa Vasileva,Karin Sim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）在机器翻译中的过生成现象及其检测策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于LLM的生成特性，其在机器翻译中可能出现各种过生成问题，影响翻译质量，因此需要识别和分类这些现象。

Method: 研究者在商业应用背景下探索了多种检测和分析LLM过生成的策略。

Result: 提出了针对不同类型过生成（如自我解释、虚构内容和合理解释）的识别方法，并展示了实际应用结果。

Conclusion: 准确识别和理解LLM的过生成行为对于提升机器翻译的可靠性和可读性具有重要意义。

Abstract: LLMs are proving to be adept at machine translation although due to their generative nature they may at times overgenerate in various ways. These overgenerations are different from the neurobabble seen in NMT and range from LLM self-explanations, to risky confabulations, to appropriate explanations, where the LLM is able to act as a human translator would, enabling greater comprehension for the target audience. Detecting and determining the exact nature of the overgenerations is a challenging task. We detail different strategies we have explored for our work in a commercial setting, and present our results.

</details>


### [135] [MADE: A Living Benchmark for Multi-Label Text Classification with Uncertainty Quantification of Medical Device Adverse Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15203)
*Raunak Agarwal,Markus Wenzel,Simon Baur,Jonas Zimmer,George Harvey,Jackie Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MADE，一个源自医疗设备不良事件报告的、持续更新的多标签文本分类基准，用于防止数据污染，并支持可靠不确定性量化（UQ）评估。研究在超过20种模型上建立了基线，揭示了不同模型在准确性与UQ之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLTC基准日益饱和且易受训练数据污染影响，难以区分真实推理能力与记忆效应；同时高风险领域需要可靠的不确定性量化以支持人工监督。

Method: 构建了一个基于医疗设备不良事件报告的动态多标签分类基准MADE，采用严格的时间划分和长尾分布的分层标签结构；在多种编码器/解码器模型上进行微调与少样本实验，并系统评估基于熵、一致性及自述置信度的不确定性量化方法。

Result: 小型判别式微调解码器在准确率和UQ之间表现最佳；生成式微调提供最可靠的UQ；大型推理模型对罕见标签表现更好但UQ较弱；自述置信度不能可靠反映不确定性。

Conclusion: MADE是一个防污染、可复现、面向高风险领域的动态MLTC基准，有助于推动兼具高性能与可靠不确定性的模型发展。

Abstract: Machine learning in high-stakes domains such as healthcare requires not only strong predictive performance but also reliable uncertainty quantification (UQ) to support human oversight. Multi-label text classification (MLTC) is a central task in this domain, yet remains challenging due to label imbalances, dependencies, and combinatorial complexity. Existing MLTC benchmarks are increasingly saturated and may be affected by training data contamination, making it difficult to distinguish genuine reasoning capabilities from memorization. We introduce MADE, a living MLTC benchmark derived from {m}edical device {ad}verse {e}vent reports and continuously updated with newly published reports to prevent contamination. MADE features a long-tailed distribution of hierarchical labels and enables reproducible evaluation with strict temporal splits. We establish baselines across more than 20 encoder- and decoder-only models under fine-tuning and few-shot settings (instruction-tuned/reasoning variants, local/API-accessible). We systematically assess entropy-/consistency-based and self-verbalized UQ methods. Results show clear trade-offs: smaller discriminatively fine-tuned decoders achieve the strongest head-to-tail accuracy while maintaining competitive UQ; generative fine-tuning delivers the most reliable UQ; large reasoning models improve performance on rare labels yet exhibit surprisingly weak UQ; and self-verbalized confidence is not a reliable proxy for uncertainty. Our work is publicly available at https://hhi.fraunhofer.de/aml-demonstrator/made-benchmark.

</details>


### [136] [From Tokens to Steps: Verification-Aware Speculative Decoding for Efficient Multi-Step Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15244)
*Kiran Purohit,Ramasuri Narayanam,Soumyabrata Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SpecGuard是一种验证感知的推测解码框架，通过仅使用模型内部信号进行步骤级验证来提高大语言模型推理的准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测解码方法由于其逐令牌特性容易导致错误传播，且依赖外部奖励模型带来额外延迟和计算开销，限制了泛化能力。

Method: SpecGuard在每一步采样多个草案候选，并选择最一致的步骤；利用基于注意力的归因分数和基于对数概率的置信度分数两种轻量级内部信号联合判断是否接受该步骤或重新计算。

Result: 实验表明，SpecGuard在多种推理基准上比传统推测解码和奖励引导的推测解码平均提升3.6%的准确性，并降低约11%的延迟。

Conclusion: SpecGuard通过内部信号实现高效的步骤级验证，在不依赖外部模型的情况下显著提升了推测解码的性能与可靠性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model inference by allowing a lightweight draft model to propose outputs that a stronger target model verifies. However, its token-centric nature allows erroneous steps to propagate. Prior approaches mitigate this using external reward models, but incur additional latency, computational overhead, and limit generalizability. We propose SpecGuard, a verification-aware speculative decoding framework that performs step-level verification using only model-internal signals. At each step, SpecGuard samples multiple draft candidates and selects the most consistent step, which is then validated using an ensemble of two lightweight model-internal signals: (i) an attention-based grounding score that measures attribution to the input and previously accepted steps, and (ii) a log-probability-based score that captures token-level confidence. These signals jointly determine whether a step is accepted or recomputed using the target, allocating compute selectively. Experiments across a range of reasoning benchmarks show that SpecGuard improves accuracy by 3.6% while reducing latency by ~11%, outperforming both SD and reward-guided SD.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [CART: Context-Aware Terrain Adaptation using Temporal Sequence Selection for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14344)
*Kartikeya Singh,Youngjin Kim,Yash Turkar,Karthik Dantu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CART的上下文感知地形适应方法，通过融合本体感知和外部感知信息，提升腿式机器人在复杂地形上的运动稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的地形适应方法依赖视觉先验，容易陷入“视觉-触感悖论”，即看到的与实际感受到的不一致，导致在复杂非结构化地形上表现不佳。

Method: 提出CART框架，结合来自机载传感器的本体感知（如振动、力反馈）和外部感知（如视觉）信息，构建对地形的鲁棒理解，并作为高层控制器指导运动控制。

Result: 在IsaacSim仿真环境中的ANYmal-C机器人和真实世界中的波士顿动力Spot机器人上进行了验证；使用基座振动稳定性作为评估指标；相比现有先进方法，仿真中平均成功率提高5%，现实中稳定性的提升分别达到45%和24%，且未增加任务完成时间。

Conclusion: 融合多模态感知的上下文感知方法能有效缓解视觉先验带来的局限性，显著提升腿式机器人在未知复杂地形上的行走稳定性和任务成功率。

Abstract: Animals in nature combine multiple modalities, such as sight and feel, to perceive terrain and develop an understanding of how to walk on uneven terrain in a stable manner. Similarly, legged robots need to develop their ability to stably walk on complex terrains by developing an understanding of the relationship between vision and proprioception. Most current terrain adaptation methods are susceptible to failure on complex, off-road terrain as they rely on prior experience, particularly observations from a vision sensor. This experience-based learning often creates a Visual-Texture Paradox between what has been seen and how it actually feels. In this work, we introduce CART, a high-level controller built on a context-aware terrain adaptation approach that integrates proprioception and exteroception from onboard sensing to achieve a robust understanding of terrain. We evaluate our method on multiple terrains using an ANYmal-C robot on the IsaacSim simulator and a Boston Dynamics SPOT robot for our real-world experiments. To evaluate the learned contextual terrain properties, we adapt vibrational stability on the base of the robot as a metric. We compare CART with various state-of-the-art baselines equipped with multimodal sensing in both simulation and the real world. CART achieves an average success rate improvement of 5% over all baselines in simulation and improves the overall stability up to 45% and 24% in the real world without increasing the time taken by the robot to accomplish locomotion tasks.

</details>


### [138] [RoSLAC: Robust Simultaneous Localization and Calibration of Multiple Magnetometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14353)
*Qiyang Lyu,Zhenyu Wu,Wei Wang,Hongming Shen,Danwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RoSLAC的鲁棒同时定位与校准方法，通过交替优化算法迭代估计平台位姿和磁力计校准参数，解决了在GPS信号弱或不可用环境下自主移动机器人因传感器平台铁磁材料导致磁力计测量失真而影响定位可靠性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS信号弱或不可用的封闭环境中，现有基于基础设施或几何特征的定位方法存在成本高、灵活性差或易受动态障碍干扰的问题，而基于环境磁场的定位虽具潜力，但磁力计易受平台自身铁磁材料干扰，需进行准确校准。传统校准方法对大型设备不适用，因此需要一种适用于实际AMR系统的高效在线校准与定位方法。

Method: 提出RoSLAC方法，采用交替优化策略，在不依赖外部基础设施的情况下，联合优化机器人位姿和磁力计畸变参数。该方法通过分解问题为两个子问题交替求解，提升计算效率，并增强对噪声和动态干扰的鲁棒性。

Result: 在高保真仿真和真实环境中进行了广泛评估，结果表明RoSLAC相比现有最先进的磁力计校准技术，在保持低计算开销的同时实现了更高的定位精度。

Conclusion: RoSLAC为自主移动机器人在复杂室内环境下的高精度、低成本磁场定位提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案，特别适用于无法旋转传感器平台的大规模系统。

Abstract: Localization of autonomous mobile robots (AMRs) in enclosed or semi-enclosed environments such as offices, hotels, hospitals, indoor parking facilities, and underground spaces where GPS signals are weak or unavailable remains a major obstacle to the deployment of fully autonomous systems. Infrastructure-based localization approaches, such as QR codes and RFID, are constrained by high installation and maintenance costs as well as limited flexibility, while onboard sensor-based methods, including LiDAR- and vision-based solutions, are affected by ambiguous geometric features and frequent occlusions caused by dynamic obstacles such as pedestrians. Ambient magnetic field (AMF)-based localization has therefore attracted growing interest in recent years because it does not rely on external infrastructure or geometric features, making it well-suited for AMR applications such as service robots and security robots. However, magnetometer measurements are often corrupted by distortions caused by ferromagnetic materials present on the sensor platform, which bias the AMF and degrade localization reliability. As a result, accurate magnetometer calibration to estimate distortion parameters becomes essential. Conventional calibration methods that rely on rotating the magnetometer are impractical for large and heavy platforms. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a robust simultaneous localization and calibration (RoSLAC) approach based on alternating optimization, which iteratively and efficiently estimates both the platform pose and magnetometer calibration parameters. Extensive evaluations conducted in high-fidelity simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that the proposed RoSLAC method achieves high localization accuracy while maintaining low computational cost compared with state-of-the-art magnetometer calibration techniques.

</details>


### [139] [BIEVR-LIO: Robust LiDAR-Inertial Odometry through Bump-Image-Enhanced Voxel Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14421)
*Patrick Pfreundschuh,Turcan Tuna,Cedric Le Gentil,Roland Siegwart,Cesar Cadena,Helen Oleynikova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为BIEVR-LIO的新型激光-惯性里程计方法，通过高分辨率地图表示和地图引导的采样策略，在几何信息稀疏的环境中显著提升了定位精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂或几何信息稀少的环境中，传统LiDAR-Inertial Odometry（LIO）方法容易因缺乏足够的约束而导致精度下降甚至发散，因此需要更鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种将表面存储为体素级定向高度图像的高分辨率地图表示方法，并设计地图引导的点云采样策略，聚焦于几何信息丰富的区域进行配准，避免中间几何特征计算，支持高效更新并降低计算开销。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在多种传感器、平台和环境中均达到先进水平，在结构良好的场景中表现优异，在挑战性场景中显著优于基线方法且未出现发散；同时生成的地图可用于高程建图等下游任务。

Conclusion: BIEVR-LIO通过细粒度几何建模和智能采样策略，有效提升了LiDAR-Inertial Odometry在低信息环境中的鲁棒性和实用性，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Reliable odometry is essential for mobile robots as they increasingly enter more challenging environments, which often contain little information to constrain point cloud registration, resulting in degraded LiDAR-Inertial Odometry (LIO) accuracy or even divergence. To address this, we present BIEVR-LIO, a novel approach designed specifically to exploit subtle variations in the available geometry for improved robustness. We propose a high-resolution map representation that stores surfaces as compact voxel-wise oriented height images. This representation can directly be used for registration without the calculation of intermediate geometric primitives while still supporting efficient updates. Since informative geometry is often sparsely distributed in the environment, we further propose a map-informed point sampling strategy to focus registration on geometrically informative regions, improving robustness in uninformative environments while reducing computational cost compared to global high-resolution sampling. Experiments across multiple sensors, platforms, and environments demonstrates state-of-the-art performance in well-constrained scenes and substantial improvements in challenging scenarios where baseline methods diverge. Additionally, we demonstrate that the fine-grained geometry captured by BIEVR-LIO can be used for downstream tasks such as elevation mapping for robot locomotion.

</details>


### [140] [CooperDrive: Enhancing Driving Decisions Through Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14454)
*Deyuan Qu,Qi Chen,Takayuki Shimizu,Onur Altintas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CooperDrive是一个协作感知框架，通过轻量级的对象级信息共享与融合，提升自动驾驶车辆在遮挡和非视距场景下的态势感知能力，实现更早、更安全的驾驶决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在遮挡和非视距（NLOS）场景下面临感知局限，导致反应延迟和碰撞风险增加，需要提升其环境感知与预判能力。

Method: CooperDrive复用检测器的鸟瞰图（BEV）特征来估计车辆位姿，重建BEV表示，并采用轻量级对象级共享与融合策略，在不改变车辆原有感知与规划系统的基础上实现协同感知。

Result: 在真实闭环测试中，CooperDrive显著提升了反应提前时间、最小碰撞时间（TTC）和制动裕度，仅需90 kbps带宽并保持平均89 ms的端到端延迟。

Conclusion: CooperDrive能有效增强自动驾驶车辆在复杂场景下的安全性与响应能力，同时具备低通信开销和低延迟优势，适用于实际部署。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles equipped with robust onboard perception, localization, and planning still face limitations in occlusion and non-line-of-sight (NLOS) scenarios, where delayed reactions can increase collision risk. We propose CooperDrive, a cooperative perception framework that augments situational awareness and enables earlier, safer driving decisions. CooperDrive offers two key advantages: (i) each vehicle retains its native perception, localization, and planning stack, and (ii) a lightweight object-level sharing and fusion strategy bridges perception and planning. Specifically, CooperDrive reuses detector Bird's-Eye View (BEV) features to estimate accurate vehicle poses without additional heavy encoders, thereby reconstructing BEV representations and feeding the planner with low latency. On the planning side, CooperDrive leverages the expanded object set to anticipate potential conflicts earlier and adjust speed and trajectory proactively, thereby transforming reactive behaviors into predictive and safer driving decisions. Real-world closed-loop tests at occlusion-heavy NLOS intersections demonstrate that CooperDrive increases reaction lead time, minimum time-to-collision (TTC), and stopping margin, while requiring only 90 kbps bandwidth and maintaining an average end-to-end latency of 89 ms.

</details>


### [141] [CT-VIR: Continuous-Time Visual-Inertial-Ranging Fusion for Indoor Localization with Sparse Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14545)
*Yu-An Liu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于样条的连续时间状态估计方法，用于视觉-惯性-测距（VIR）融合定位，通过VIO运动先验和UWB测距构建虚拟锚点并优化轨迹，提升了多传感器异步融合下的定位精度与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VIO系统长期使用会产生漂移，引入UWB等测距传感器可缓解该问题，但传统方法依赖离散时间滤波或优化，难以在异步采样下兼顾精度、一致性和效率；同时高质量测距需要良好部署的锚点，在实际环境中难以保证。

Method: 在预处理阶段利用VIO运动先验生成虚拟锚点并剔除UWB异常值；在估计阶段采用B样条对位姿轨迹进行连续时间参数化，并将惯性、视觉和测距约束建模为滑动窗口图中的因子，联合优化样条控制点和辅助参数以获得连续轨迹估计。

Result: 在公开数据集和真实实验中验证了所提方法的有效性，相较于传统离散方法在定位精度、轨迹平滑性和多传感器融合效率方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 该方法有效缓解了VIO的长期漂移问题，提高了UWB测距的鲁棒性与可用性，实现了高精度、高一致性的连续时间VIR融合定位，适用于复杂部署环境下的移动机器人应用。

Abstract: Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) is widely used for mobile robot localization, but its long-term accuracy degrades without global constraints. Incorporating ranging sensors such as ultra-wideband (UWB) can mitigate drift; however, high-accuracy ranging usually requires well-deployed anchors, which is difficult to ensure in narrow or low-power environments. Moreover, most existing visual-inertial-ranging (VIR) fusion methods rely on discrete time-based filtering or optimization, making it difficult to balance positioning accuracy, trajectory consistency, and fusion efficiency under asynchronous multi-sensor sampling. To address these issues, we propose a spline-based continuous-time state estimation method for VIR fusion localization. In the preprocessing stage, VIO motion priors and UWB ranging measurements are used to construct virtual anchors and reject outliers, thereby alleviating geometric degeneration and improving range reliability. In the estimation stage, the pose trajectory is parameterized in continuous time using a B-spline, while inertial, visual, and ranging constraints are formulated as factors in a sliding-window graph. The spline control points, together with a small set of auxiliary parameters, are then jointly optimized to obtain a continuous-time trajectory estimate. Evaluations on public datasets and real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and practical potential of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [142] [Model-Based Reinforcement Learning Exploits Passive Body Dynamics for High-Performance Biped Robot Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14565)
*Tomoya Kamimura,Haruka Washiyama,Akihito Sano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究利用基于模型的深度强化学习，探讨了被动元件（如弹簧）在双足机器人行走和奔跑运动生成中的作用，发现具有被动元件的机器人能通过身体与地面的动态交互产生稳定的极限环，从而实现高效、节能且鲁棒的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索机器人身体的被动性如何影响其运动学习过程，强调具身智能中物理结构对运动控制的重要性。

Method: 在仿真环境中构建了两种双足机器人模型：一种包含被动元件（如弹簧），另一种为传统无被动元件的人形模型，并采用基于模型的深度强化学习进行训练。

Result: 具有被动元件的模型虽初期奖励增长慢，但因系统吸引子的作用，轨迹快速收敛至稳定极限环，最终获得更鲁棒、节能的高性能运动表现。

Conclusion: 被动元件通过形成稳定的动力学吸引子，提升了运动学习的效率与鲁棒性，表明在具身AI中设计具备被动特性的身体结构至关重要。

Abstract: Embodiment is a significant keyword in recent machine learning fields. This study focused on the passive nature of the body of a biped robot to generate walking and running locomotion using model-based deep reinforcement learning. We constructed two models in a simulator, one with passive elements (e.g., springs) and the other, which is similar to general humanoids, without passive elements. The training of the model with passive elements was highly affected by the attractor of the system. This lead that although the trajectories quickly converged to limit cycles, it took a long time to obtain large rewards. However, thanks to the attractor-driven learning, the acquired locomotion was robust and energy-efficient. The results revealed that robots with passive elements could efficiently acquire high-performance locomotion by utilizing stable limit cycles generated through dynamic interaction between the body and ground. This study demonstrates the importance of implementing passive properties in the body for future embodied AI.

</details>


### [143] [A multi-platform LiDAR dataset for standardized forest inventory measurement at long term ecological monitoring sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14635)
*Michael R. Chang,Anna Candotti,Karl von Ellenrieder,Enrico Tomelleri,Marco Camurri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提供了一个多平台LiDAR参考数据集，整合了无人机、地面和背包移动激光扫描数据，用于森林三维结构与生态观测的结合，支持校准、基准测试和森林数字孪生构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了支持3D结构数据与生态观测及标准异速生长模型的整合，需要一个高质量、多平台的LiDAR数据集用于校准和基准测试。

Method: 结合无人机激光扫描（ULS）、地面激光扫描（TLS）和背包移动激光扫描（MLS），采用无标记物、SLAM感知的协议，并结合手动与自动化方法进行数据配准与处理。

Result: 获得了包含约3.33亿点云的TLS数据，并补充了ULS和MLS数据，覆盖树冠和林下层；数据以LAZ和E57格式公开，附带配准报告，支持可重复研究。

Conclusion: 该数据集为测试配准方法、评估扫描效率以及连接点云与生物量估算等生态模型提供了基准，并展示了多平台LiDAR在森林长期监测和数字孪生中的应用潜力。

Abstract: We present a curated multi-platform LiDAR reference dataset from an instrumented ICOS forest plot, explicitly designed to support calibration, benchmarking, and integration of 3D structural data with ecological observations and standard allometric models. The dataset integrates UAV-borne laser scanning (ULS) to measure canopy coverage, terrestrial laser scanning (TLS) for detailed stem mapping, and backpack mobile laser scanning (MLS) with real-time SLAM for efficient sub-canopy acquisition. We focus on the control plot with the most complete and internally consistent registration, where TLS point clouds (~333 million points) are complemented by ULS and MLS data capturing canopy and understory strata. Marker-free, SLAM-aware protocols were used to reduce field and processing time, while manual and automated methods were combined. Final products are available in LAZ and E57 formats with UTM coordinates, together with registration reports for reproducibility. The dataset provides a benchmark for testing registration methods, evaluating scanning efficiency, and linking point clouds with segmentation, quantitative structure models, and allometric biomass estimation. By situating the acquisitions at a long-term ICOS site, it is explicitly linked to 3D structure with decades of ecological and flux measurements. More broadly, it illustrates how TLS, MLS, and ULS can be combined for repeated inventories and digital twins of forest ecosystems.

</details>


### [144] [DigiForest: Digital Analytics and Robotics for Sustainable Forestry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14652)
*Marco Camurri,Enrico Tomelleri,Matías Mattamala,Sebastián Barbas Laina,Martin Jacquet,Jens Behley,Sunni Kanta Prasad Kushwaha,Fang Nan,Nived Chebrolu,Leonard Freißmuth,Marvin Chayton Harms,Meher V. R. Malladi,Fan Yang,Jonas Frey,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter,Janine Schweier,Kostas Alexis,Cyrill Stachniss,Maurice Fallon,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DigiForest是一个利用数字技术和自主机器人技术的新型大规模精准林业方法，旨在通过自动化数据采集、森林生长预测和低影响选择性采伐来促进欧洲森林的可持续管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现欧盟的气候中和与生物多样性目标，需要创新当前森林管理实践，以应对可持续森林管理、增加长效木材产品使用和增强森林生态系统韧性的挑战。

Method: DigiForest包含四个主要组成部分：（1）用于树木级数据收集的自主异构移动机器人（空中、足式和袋状机器人）；（2）自动提取树木特征以构建森林清查；（3）用于预测森林生长和支持决策的决策支持系统（DSS）；（4）使用专用自主采伐机进行低影响选择性采伐。这些技术已在芬兰、英国和瑞士等多个实地环境中广泛验证。

Result: 该方法在多个真实森林环境中得到了验证，展示了其在提升森林管理精度和可持续性方面的潜力。

Conclusion: DigiForest为实现欧洲森林的可持续管理和气候目标提供了创新且可扩展的技术解决方案。

Abstract: Covering one third of Earth's land surface, forests are vital to global biodiversity, climate regulation, and human well-being. In Europe, forests and woodlands reach approximately 40% of land area, and the forestry sector is central to achieving the EU's climate neutrality and biodiversity goals; these emphasize sustainable forest management, increased use of long-lived wood products, and resilient forest ecosystems. To meet these goals and properly address their inherent challenges, current practices require further innovation. This chapter introduces DigiForest, a novel, large-scale precision forestry approach leveraging digital technologies and autonomous robotics. DigiForest is structured around four main components: (1) autonomous, heterogeneous mobile robots (aerial, legged, and marsupial) for tree-level data collection; (2) automated extraction of tree traits to build forest inventories; (3) a Decision Support System (DSS) for forecasting forest growth and supporting decision-making; and (4) low-impact selective logging using purpose-built autonomous harvesters. These technologies have been extensively validated in real-world conditions in several locations, including forests in Finland, the UK, and Switzerland.

</details>


### [145] [World-Value-Action Model: Implicit Planning for Vision-Language-Action Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14732)
*Runze Li,Hongyin Zhang,Junxi Jin,Qixin Zeng,Zifeng Zhuang,Yiqi Tang,Shangke Lyu,Donglin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为World-Value-Action (WAV) 的统一框架，用于在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）系统中实现隐式规划，通过在潜在空间中进行推理来提升长视野决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型多依赖直接动作预测，缺乏对长视野轨迹的推理和后果评估能力，限制了其在复杂任务中的表现。

Method: WAV模型利用学习到的世界模型预测未来状态，并通过轨迹价值函数评估长期效用；动作生成被建模为在条件于视觉和语言输入的潜在轨迹空间中的推理过程，使概率质量逐渐集中于高价值且动态可行的轨迹上。

Result: 理论分析表明，直接在动作空间规划会导致可行轨迹概率随视野增长呈指数衰减，而潜在空间推理能重塑搜索分布，提高效率；实验结果显示WAV在模拟和真实场景中均优于现有方法，显著提升了任务成功率、泛化性和鲁棒性，尤其在长视野和组合性任务中表现突出。

Conclusion: WAV提供了一种有效的隐式规划机制，克服了传统VLA模型在长视野决策中的局限，推动了具身智能体在复杂环境中的应用。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for building embodied agents that ground perception and language into action. However, most existing approaches rely on direct action prediction, lacking the ability to reason over long-horizon trajectories and evaluate their consequences, which limits performance in complex decision-making tasks. In this work, we introduce World-Value-Action (WAV) model, a unified framework that enables implicit planning in VLA systems. Rather than performing explicit trajectory optimization, WAV model learn a structured latent representation of future trajectories conditioned on visual observations and language instructions. A learned world model predicts future states, while a trajectory value function evaluates their long-horizon utility. Action generation is then formulated as inference in this latent space, where the model progressively concentrates probability mass on high-value and dynamically feasible trajectories. We provide a theoretical perspective showing that planning directly in action space suffers from an exponential decay in the probability of feasible trajectories as the horizon increases. In contrast, latent-space inference reshapes the search distribution toward feasible regions, enabling efficient long-horizon decision making. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate that the WAV model consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving significant improvements in task success rate, generalization ability, and robustness, especially in long-horizon and compositional scenarios.

</details>


### [146] [Differentiable Object Pose Connectivity Metrics for Regrasp Sequence Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14733)
*Liang Qin,Weiwei Wan,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于可微姿态序列连通性度量的隐式多步重抓取规划框架，通过能量模型实现抓取可行性的连续优化，并引入自适应迭代加深策略自动确定最小中间步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重抓取规划在推理中间姿态的共享抓取连通性时，离散搜索方法容易变得脆弱，难以保证规划鲁棒性。

Method: 使用基于能量的模型（EBM）建模物体姿态下的抓取可行性，利用能量可加性构建连续的能量景观以衡量姿态对之间的连通性，并结合梯度优化中间物体姿态；引入自适应迭代加深策略自动确定所需的最小中间步数。

Result: 实验表明所提成本函数能提供平滑且信息丰富的梯度，相比其他方法提升了规划鲁棒性；模型能泛化到未见过的抓取姿态，并实现跨末端执行器迁移（如从吸盘约束训练迁移到平行夹爪操作）；验证了自适应加深与最小步数搜索的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过连续优化与自适应策略实现了更鲁棒、泛化性更强的多步重抓取规划，为复杂抓取任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Regrasp planning is often required when one pick-and-place cannot transfer an object from an initial pose to a goal pose while maintaining grasp feasibility. The main challenge is to reason about shared-grasp connectivity across intermediate poses, where discrete search becomes brittle. We propose an implicit multi-step regrasp planning framework based on differentiable pose sequence connectivity metrics. We model grasp feasibility under an object pose using an Energy-Based Model (EBM) and leverage energy additivity to construct a continuous energy landscape that measures pose-pair connectivity, enabling gradient-based optimization of intermediate object poses. An adaptive iterative deepening strategy is introduced to determine the minimum number of intermediate steps automatically. Experiments show that the proposed cost formulation provides smooth and informative gradients, improving planning robustness over other alternatives. They also demonstrate generalization to unseen grasp poses and cross-end-effector transfer, where a model trained with suction constraints can guide parallel gripper grasp manipulation. The multi-step planning results further highlight the effectiveness of adaptive deepening and minimum-step search.

</details>


### [147] [Keep It CALM: Toward Calibration-Free Kilometer-Level SLAM with Visual Geometry Foundation Models via an Assistant Eye](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14795)
*Tianjun Zhang,Fengyi Zhang,Tianchen Deng,Lin Zhang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CAL2M的即插即用框架，用于解决视觉几何基础模型（VGFMs）在千米级SLAM中因非线性几何失真导致的轨迹漂移和地图发散问题。该方法引入“辅助眼”消除尺度模糊，并通过极线引导的内参与位姿校正及基于锚点传播的全局一致映射策略实现高精度大范围定位与建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VGFMs在大规模SLAM应用中依赖线性变换进行子图对齐，无法有效建模其输出中存在的复杂非线性几何失真，导致误差累积和地图不一致。因此需要一种更灵活、无需标定的方法来提升大尺度重建的精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出CAL2M框架：1）引入‘辅助眼’利用物理间距恒定先验，消除尺度歧义且无需时空标定；2）设计基于极线引导的内参与位姿校正模型，结合在线内参搜索模块，通过基础矩阵分解修正由内参不准引起的旋转和平移误差；3）采用基于锚点传播的全局一致映射策略，构建并融合轨迹上的锚点，建立局部到全局的直接映射关系，支持非线性变换以弹性对齐子图。

Result: CAL2M能够在无需标定的情况下有效纠正VGFMs输出中的非线性几何失真，显著减少轨迹漂移和地图分裂，在千米级SLAM任务中实现了更高精度和全局一致性的重建效果。

Conclusion: CAL2M为基于VGFMs的大规模定位与建图提供了一个通用、高效的解决方案，突破了传统线性对齐方法的局限，展示了在实际场景中部署VGFMs的可行性。

Abstract: Visual Geometry Foundation Models (VGFMs) demonstrate remarkable zero-shot capabilities in local reconstruction. However, deploying them for kilometer-level Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) remains challenging. In such scenarios, current approaches mainly rely on linear transforms (e.g., Sim3 and SL4) for sub-map alignment, while we argue that a single linear transform is fundamentally insufficient to model the complex, non-linear geometric distortions inherent in VGFM outputs. Forcing such rigid alignment leads to the rapid accumulation of uncorrected residuals, eventually resulting in significant trajectory drift and map divergence. To address these limitations, we present CAL2M (Calibration-free Assistant-eye based Large-scale Localization and Mapping), a plug-and-play framework compatible with arbitrary VGFMs. Distinct from traditional systems, CAL2M introduces an "assistant eye" solely to leverage the prior of constant physical spacing, effectively eliminating scale ambiguity without any temporal or spatial pre-calibration. Furthermore, leveraging the assumption of accurate feature matching, we propose an epipolar-guided intrinsic and pose correction model. Supported by an online intrinsic search module, it can effectively rectify rotation and translation errors caused by inaccurate intrinsics through fundamental matrix decomposition. Finally, to ensure accurate mapping, we introduce a globally consistent mapping strategy based on anchor propagation. By constructing and fusing anchors across the trajectory, we establish a direct local-to-global mapping relationship. This enables the application of nonlinear transformations to elastically align sub-maps, effectively eliminating geometric misalignments and ensuring a globally consistent reconstruction. The source code of CAL2M will be publicly available at https://github.com/IRMVLab/CALM.

</details>


### [148] [Switch: Learning Agile Skills Switching for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14834)
*Yuen-Fui Lau,Qihan Zhao,Yinhuai Wang,Runyi Yu,Hok Wai Tsui,Qifeng Chen,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Switch的分层多技能系统，通过技能图、基于深度强化学习的全身跟踪策略和在线技能调度器，实现了人形机器人在不同运动技能间的无缝切换，显著提升了复杂运动控制中的灵活性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全身控制方法在不同技能之间灵活切换时存在困难，导致安全性问题和实际应用限制，因此需要一种能够实现平滑、实时技能转换的解决方案。

Method: 提出一个包含技能图（SG）、基于深度强化学习训练的全身跟踪策略以及在线技能调度器的三层架构；利用运动数据中的运动学相似性构建技能图，并通过在线图搜索实现最优路径规划以支持动态切换。

Result: 实验表明，Switch系统能高效、稳定地执行多种运动技能之间的敏捷切换，具有高成功率，同时保持良好的运动模仿性能。

Conclusion: Switch为实现人形机器人在真实环境中复杂且连续的运动控制提供了有效框架，显著增强了多技能协同与实时适应能力。

Abstract: Recent advancements in whole-body control through deep reinforcement learning have enabled humanoid robots to achieve remarkable progress in real-world chal lenging locomotion skills. However, existing approaches often struggle with flexible transitions between distinct skills, cre ating safety concerns and practical limitations. To address this challenge, we introduce a hierarchical multi-skill system, Switch, enabling seamless skill transitions at any moment. Our approach comprises three key components: (1) a Skill Graph (SG) that establishes potential cross-skill transitions based on kinematic similarity within multi-skill motion data, (2) a whole-body tracking policy trained on this skill graph through deep reinforcement learning, and (3) an online skill scheduler to drive the tracking policy for robust skill execution and smooth transitions. For skill switching or significant tracking deviations, the scheduler performs online graph search to find the optimal feasible path, which ensures efficient, stable, and real-time execution of diverse locomotion skills. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that Switch empowers humanoid to execute agile skill transitions with high success rates while maintaining strong motion imitation performance.

</details>


### [149] [Graph Theoretical Outlier Rejection for 4D Radar Registration in Feature-Poor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14857)
*Georg Dorndorf,Daniel Adolfsson,Masrur Doostdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图的配对一致性最大化方法（PCM）用于4D成像雷达点云配准，通过引入雷达自适应的距离不变评分函数和各向异性不确定性建模，在开放矿山环境中显著降低了相对位姿误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低可见度和特征稀疏的环境中（如露天矿），传统ICP算法因噪声、多径反射和缺乏明显特征导致配准困难，需要提高对应点匹配的鲁棒性。

Method: 将图-based PCM作为外点剔除步骤嵌入ICP循环中，设计了适用于雷达的配对距离不变评分函数，并结合基于雷达测量模型的各向异性检测不确定性；使用贪心启发式算法求解最大团以近似一致性最大化问题。

Result: 在真实露天矿4D雷达数据集上测试，相比无PCM的GICP基线，1米段RPE降低29.6%，100米段最高降低55%。

Conclusion: 该方法显著提升了恶劣环境下雷达点云配准的精度与鲁棒性，适用于在线定位系统集成。

Abstract: Automotive 4D imaging radar is well suited for operation in dusty and low-visibility environments, but scan registration remains challenging due to scan sparsity and spurious detections caused by noise and multipath reflections. This difficulty is compounded in feature-poor open-pit mines, where the lack of distinctive landmarks reduces correspondence reliability. We integrate graph-based pairwise consistency maximization (PCM) as an outlier rejection step within the iterative closest points (ICP) loop. We propose a radar-adapted pairwise distance-invariant scoring function for graph-based (PCM) that incorporates anisotropic, per-detection uncertainty derived from a radar measurement model. The consistency maximization problem is approximated with a greedy heuristic that finds a large clique in the pairwise consistency graph. The refined correspondence set improves robustness when the initial association set is heavily contaminated. We evaluate a standard Euclidean distance residual and our uncertainty-aware residual on an open-pit mine dataset collected with a 4D imaging radar. Compared to the generalized ICP (GICP) baseline without PCM, our method reduces segment relative position error (RPE) by 29.6% on 1 m segments and by up to 55% on 100 m segments. The presented method is intended for integration into localization pipelines and is suitable for online use due to the greedy heuristic in graph-based (PCM).

</details>


### [150] [4D Radar Gaussian Modeling and Scan Matching with RCS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14868)
*Fernando Amodeo,Luis Merino,Fernando Caballero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在4D毫米波雷达扫描匹配中引入雷达截面（RCS）物理特性以增强场景建模和匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常忽略雷达截面（RCS）信息，未能充分利用4D毫米波雷达的全部感知潜力。

Method: 在3D高斯建模基础上，将RCS的物理行为纳入建模过程，丰富场景表征并改进扫描匹配算法。

Result: 通过融合RCS信息，提升了扫描匹配的精度与鲁棒性，增强了对环境的感知能力。

Conclusion: 引入RCS物理特性有助于提升4D毫米波雷达在机器人应用中的建模与匹配性能。

Abstract: 4D millimeter-wave (mmWave) radars are increasingly used in robotics, as they offer robustness against adverse environmental conditions. Besides the usual XYZ position, they provide Doppler velocity measurements as well as Radar Cross Section (RCS) information for every point. While Doppler is widely used to filter out dynamic points, RCS is often overlooked and not usually used in modeling and scan matching processes. Building on previous 3D Gaussian modeling and scan matching work, we propose incorporating the physical behavior of RCS in the model, in order to further enrich the summarized information about the scene, and improve the scan matching process.

</details>


### [151] [An Intelligent Robotic and Bio-Digestor Framework for Smart Waste Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14882)
*Radhika Khatri,Adit Tewari,Nikhil Sharma,M. B. Srinivas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成的智能废物管理框架，结合机器人垃圾分类模块和优化的生物消化器系统，实现高效、自动化的市政固体废物处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市化和人口增长使市政固体废物管理日益困难，传统方法依赖人工且效率低，亟需智能化、自动化的解决方案。

Method: 采用MyCobot 280 Jetson Nano机械臂结合YOLOv8目标检测和基于ROS的路径规划实现垃圾实时识别与分类；可降解垃圾送入配备多传感器的生物消化器，利用PSO算法结合回归模型动态优化温度、pH、压力等参数。

Result: 系统在动态条件下测试显示垃圾分类准确率达98%，生物转化效率高，运行稳定。

Conclusion: 该框架为现代废物管理提供了一个可扩展、智能且实用的解决方案，适用于住宅和工业场景。

Abstract: Rapid urbanization and continuous population growth have made municipal solid waste management increasingly challenging. These challenges highlight the need for smarter and automated waste management solutions. This paper presents the design and evaluation of an integrated waste management framework that combines two connected systems, a robotic waste segregation module and an optimized bio-digestor. The robotic waste segregation system uses a MyCobot 280 Jetson Nano robotic arm along with YOLOv8 object detection and robot operating system (ROS)-based path planning to identify and sort waste in real time. It classifies waste into four different categories with high precision, reducing the need for manual intervention. After segregation, the biodegradable waste is transferred to a bio-digestor system equipped with multiple sensors. These sensors continuously monitor key parameters, including temperature, pH, pressure, and motor revolutions per minute. The Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) algorithm, combined with a regression model, is used to dynamically adjust system parameters. This intelligent optimization approach ensures stable operation and maximizes digestion efficiency under varying environmental conditions. System testing under dynamic conditions demonstrates a sorting accuracy of 98% along with highly efficient biological conversion. The proposed framework offers a scalable, intelligent, and practical solution for modern waste management, making it suitable for both residential and industrial applications.

</details>


### [152] [HRDexDB: A Large-Scale Dataset of Dexterous Human and Robotic Hand Grasps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14944)
*Jongbin Lim,Taeyun Ha,Mingi Choi,Jisoo Kim,Byungjun Kim,Subin Jeon,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HRDexDB是一个大规模、多模态的高保真灵巧抓取数据集，涵盖人类和多种机器人手在100个不同物体上的抓取序列，提供高精度3D运动轨迹、触觉信号和多视角视频，包含1.4K次抓取试验，支持跨域灵巧操作与多模态策略学习研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集缺乏跨人类与多种机器人手的高保真、多模态抓取数据，限制了灵巧操作和跨域策略学习的研究，因此需要一个统一且高精度的基准数据集。

Method: 采用先进的视觉方法和专用多相机系统采集人类与多种机器人手在相同物体和相似抓取动作下的操作数据，同步记录3D运动轨迹、触觉信号、多视角及第一人称视频，并标注成功与失败案例。

Result: 构建了包含1.4K次抓取试验的HRDexDB数据集，覆盖100个物体，提供高精度时空对齐的3D姿态、视觉、触觉和运动学多模态数据。

Conclusion: HRDexDB为多模态策略学习和跨域灵巧操作提供了坚实的数据基础，有望推动人机协同与机器人灵巧抓取的发展。

Abstract: We present HRDexDB, a large-scale, multi-modal dataset of high-fidelity dexterous grasping sequences featuring both human and diverse robotic hands. Unlike existing datasets, HRDexDB provides a comprehensive collection of grasping trajectories across human hands and multiple robot hand embodiments, spanning 100 diverse objects. Leveraging state-of-the-art vision methods and a new dedicated multi-camera system, our HRDexDB offers high-precision spatiotemporal 3D ground-truth motion for both the agent and the manipulated object. To facilitate the study of physical interaction, HRDexDB includes high-resolution tactile signals, synchronized multi-view video, and egocentric video streams. The dataset comprises 1.4K grasping trials, encompassing both successes and failures, each enriched with visual, kinematic, and tactile modalities. By providing closely aligned captures of human dexterity and robotic execution on the same target objects under comparable grasping motions, HRDexDB serves as a foundational benchmark for multi-modal policy learning and cross-domain dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [153] [Momentum-constrained Hybrid Heuristic Trajectory Optimization Framework with Residual-enhanced DRL for Visually Impaired Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14986)
*Yuting Zeng,Zhiwen Zheng,Jingya Wang,You Zhou,JiaLing Xiao,Yongbin Yu,Manping Fan,Bo Gong,Liyong Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于视障辅助规划的动量约束混合启发式轨迹优化框架（MHHTOF），通过结合启发式采样、强化学习和双阶段成本建模，实现了在安全性、舒适性、效率和可解释性方面的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在多目标优化、泛化能力和可解释性方面存在不足，难以满足视障辅助场景中安全高效的路径规划需求。

Method: 提出MHHTOF框架，包含动量约束轨迹优化的启发式轨迹采样簇（HTSC-MTO）、残差增强的深度强化学习模块以及双阶段成本建模机制（DCMM），在Frenet坐标系中保证轨迹一致性，在笛卡尔坐标系中引入奖励驱动的自适应权重以融合用户偏好。

Result: 实验表明，该框架收敛速度约为基线方法的两倍，成本更低且更稳定；在复杂动态场景中表现出平稳的速度与加速度曲线，风险更低。

Conclusion: MHHTOF在多目标平衡、鲁棒性、安全性和效率方面优于现有方法，具备良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Safe and efficient assistive planning for visually impaired scenarios remains challenging, since existing methods struggle with multi-objective optimization, generalization, and interpretability. In response, this paper proposes a Momentum-Constrained Hybrid Heuristic Trajectory Optimization Framework (MHHTOF). To balance multiple objectives of comfort and safety, the framework designs a Heuristic Trajectory Sampling Cluster (HTSC) with a Momentum-Constrained Trajectory Optimization (MTO), which suppresses abrupt velocity and acceleration changes. In addition, a novel residual-enhanced deep reinforcement learning (DRL) module refines candidate trajectories, advancing temporal modeling and policy generalization. Finally, a dual-stage cost modeling mechanism (DCMM) is introduced to regulate optimization, where costs in the Frenet space ensure consistency, and reward-driven adaptive weights in the Cartesian space integrate user preferences for interpretability and user-centric decision-making. Experimental results show that the proposed framework converges in nearly half the iterations of baselines and achieves lower and more stable costs. In complex dynamic scenarios, MHHTOF further demonstrates stable velocity and acceleration curves with reduced risk, confirming its advantages in robustness, safety, and efficiency.

</details>


### [154] [DEX-Mouse: A Low-cost Portable and Universal Interface with Force Feedback for Data Collection of Dexterous Robotic Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15013)
*Joonho Koh,Haechan Jung,Nayoung Kim,Wook Ko,Changjoo Nam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEX-Mouse是一种低成本、便携式、无需校准的手持遥操作接口，具备运动觉力反馈，支持机器人手部附着于操作员前臂的配置，生成机器人对齐的数据，提升任务完成率并降低操作负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的灵巧手操作数据采集方法存在仿真到现实的差距、标定需求、缺乏便携性或跨平台兼容性等问题，需要一种更通用、易部署的遥操作方案。

Method: 设计并实现DEX-Mouse系统，采用商用现成组件构建手持式接口，集成运动觉力反馈，支持操作员无关的即插即用使用，并支持将目标机器人手直接安装在操作员前臂上以生成对齐数据。

Result: 在用户实验中，使用所提系统的操作员在附着配置下实现了86.67%的任务完成率，且感知工作负荷低于分离式遥操作设置。系统硬件和软件栈全部开源。

Conclusion: DEX-Mouse是一种高效、便携、免校准的遥操作接口，能够生成高质量的机器人对齐操作数据，适用于多平台和多操作员场景，推动数据驱动的灵巧手操控发展。

Abstract: Data-driven dexterous hand manipulation requires large-scale, physically consistent demonstration data. Simulation and video-based methods suffer from sim-to-real gaps and retargeting problems, while MoCap glove-based teleoperation systems require per-operator calibration and lack portability, as the robot hand is typically fixed to a stationary arm. Portable alternatives improve mobility but lack cross-platform and cross-operator compatibility. We present DEX-Mouse, a portable, calibration-free hand-held teleoperation interface with integrated kinesthetic force feedback, built from commercial off-the-shelf components under USD 150. The operator-agnostic design requires no calibration or structural modification, enabling immediate deployment across diverse environments and platforms. The interface supports a configuration in which the target robot hand is mounted directly on the forearm of an operator, producing robot-aligned data. In a comparative user study across various dexterous manipulation tasks, operators using the proposed system achieved an 86.67% task completion rate under the attached configuration. Also, we found that the attached configuration reduced the perceived workload of the operators compared to spatially separated teleoperation setups across all compared interfaces. The complete hardware and software stack, including bill of materials, CAD models, and firmware, is open-sourced at https://dex-mouse.github.io/ to facilitate replication and adoption.

</details>


### [155] [DockAnywhere: Data-Efficient Visuomotor Policy Learning for Mobile Manipulation via Novel Demonstration Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15023)
*Ziyu Shan,Yuheng Zhou,Gaoyuan Wu,Ziheng Ji,Zhenyu Wu,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DockAnywhere的低成本示范生成框架，通过将单个示范扩展到多种可行的停靠配置，提升移动操作在不同停靠点下的视角泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
