Motivation: 现有移动操作方法因停靠点变化导致视觉视角不一致，难以在真实环境中泛化，本文旨在解决这一视图泛化问题。

Method: 通过解耦依赖于停靠的底座运动与跨视角不变的操作技能，在满足可行性约束下采样多个停靠点，并利用保持结构的增强方法生成对应轨迹；使用点云表示机器人和物体，通过点级空间编辑合成多视角下的视觉观测。

Result: 在ManiSkill仿真环境和真实机器人平台上实验表明，该方法显著提高了策略成功率，并能很好地泛化至训练中未见过的新停靠点视角。

Conclusion: DockAnywhere有效提升了移动操作策略在不同停靠配置下的泛化能力，为真实场景中的灵活部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation is a fundamental capability that enables robots to interact in expansive environments such as homes and factories. Most existing approaches follow a two-stage paradigm, where the robot first navigates to a docking point and then performs fixed-base manipulation using powerful visuomotor policies. However, real-world mobile manipulation often suffers from the view generalization problem due to shifts of docking points. To address this issue, we propose a novel low-cost demonstration generation framework named DockAnywhere, which improves viewpoint generalization under docking variability by lifting a single demonstration to diverse feasible docking configurations. Specifically, DockAnywhere lifts a trajectory to any feasible docking points by decoupling docking-dependent base motions from contact-rich manipulation skills that remain invariant across viewpoints. Feasible docking proposals are sampled under feasibility constraints, and corresponding trajectories are generated via structure-preserving augmentation. Visual observations are synthesized in 3D space by representing the robot and objects as point clouds and applying point-level spatial editing to ensure the consistency of observation and action across viewpoints. Extensive experiments on ManiSkill and real-world platforms demonstrate that DockAnywhere substantially improves policy success rates and easily generalizes to novel viewpoints from unseen docking points during training, significantly enhancing the generalization capability of mobile manipulation policy in real-world deployment.

</details>


### [156] [CAVERS: Multimodal SLAM Data from a Natural Karstic Cave with Ground Truth Motion Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15052)
*Giacomo Franchini,David Rodríguez-Martínez,Alfonso Martínez-Petersen,C. J. Pérez-del-Pulgar,Marcello Chiaberge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为CAVERS的多模态数据集，用于在自然喀斯特洞穴中进行机器人感知与导航研究，提供了丰富的传感器数据和高精度真值，支持多种SLAM和三维重建算法的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有公开数据集在自然喀斯特洞穴环境中的传感模态和环境多样性方面有限，难以满足自主机器人在此类复杂环境下的感知与导航研究需求，因此需要构建一个更全面、更具代表性的数据集。

Method: 在西班牙马拉加的Cueva de la Victoria洞穴中采集了两个结构不同的区域的数据，使用包括RGB-D-I相机、近红外热像仪和LiDAR在内的多传感器套件，在完全黑暗和人工照明条件下以手持和轮式机器人搭载方式采集数据，并部署OptiTrack动捕系统提供毫米级精度的6自由度真值姿态与速度信息。

Result: 共收集24个序列，约335GB数据，涵盖了多种传感器模态；对七种先进的SLAM与里程计算法及一种3D重建流程进行了基准测试，验证了数据集的可用性。

Conclusion: CAVERS数据集填补了自然喀斯特洞穴环境中多模态、高精度机器人感知数据的空白，适用于极端光照、湿滑表面和复杂几何结构下的导航算法开发与评估。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in natural karstic caves face perception and navigation challenges that are qualitatively distinct from those encountered in mines or tunnels: irregular geometry, reflective wet surfaces, near-zero ambient light, and complex branching passages. Yet publicly available datasets targeting this environment remain scarce and offer limited sensing modalities and environmental diversity. We present CAVERS, a multimodal dataset acquired in two structurally distinct rooms of Cueva de la Victoria, Málaga, Spain, comprising 24 sequences totaling approximately 335 GB of recorded data. The sensor suite combines an Intel RealSense D435i RGB-D-I camera, an Optris PI640i near-IR thermal camera, and a Velodyne VLP-16 LiDAR, operated both handheld and mounted on a wheeled rover under full darkness and artificial illumination. For most of the sequences, mm-accurate 6-DoF ground truth pose and velocity at 120 Hz are provided by an Optirack motion capture system installed directly inside the cave. We benchmark seven state-of-the-art SLAM and odometry algorithms spanning visual, visual-inertial, thermal-inertial, and LiDAR-based pipelines, as well as a 3D reconstruction pipeline, demonstrating the dataset's usability. %The dataset and all supplementary material are publicly available at: https://github.com/spaceuma/cavers.

</details>


### [157] [Trajectory Planning for a Multi-UAV Rigid-Payload Cascaded Transportation System Based on Enhanced Tube-RRT*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15074)
*Jianqiao Yu,Jia Li,Tianhua Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多无人机-刚性负载级联系统在密集障碍环境中运输的两阶段轨迹规划框架，第一阶段采用改进的Tube-RRT*算法快速生成安全平滑的虚拟通道，第二阶段通过凸二次规划生成满足动力学与约束条件的负载轨迹，并结合集中式几何控制验证了框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集障碍环境中，多无人机协同运输刚性负载面临路径规划困难、缆绳振荡和碰撞风险高等挑战，现有方法在采样效率、路径质量与系统动态约束整合方面存在不足。

Method: 第一阶段提出增强型Tube-RRT*算法，融合主动混合采样与自适应扩展策略，并在边代价中引入轨迹平滑性成本以减少转弯；第二阶段构建包含负载平动/转动动力学、缆绳张力与避障约束的凸二次规划问题，生成优化轨迹；最后采用集中式几何控制器实现系统闭环控制。

Result: 仿真表明，所提Enhanced Tube-RRT*相比STube-RRT*和AETube-RRT*具有更高的成功率与有效采样率，路径更短且累计转角更小；第二阶段生成的轨迹满足各项物理与安全约束，控制验证显示该框架能有效实现复杂环境下的负载姿态调控。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段轨迹规划框架在密集障碍环境下具备良好的可行性与有效性，能够提升路径规划效率与安全性，抑制缆绳振荡，为多无人机协同吊运提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a two-stage trajectory planning framework for a multi-UAV rigid-payload cascaded transportation system, aiming to address planning challenges in densely cluttered environments. In Stage I, an Enhanced Tube-RRT* algorithm is developed by integrating active hybrid sampling and an adaptive expansion strategy, enabling rapid generation of a safe and feasible virtual tube in environments with dense obstacles. Moreover, a trajectory smoothness cost is explicitly incorporated into the edge cost to reduce excessive turns and thereby mitigate cable-induced oscillations. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed Enhanced Tube-RRT* achieves a higher success rate and effective sampling rate than mixed-sampling Tube-RRT* (STube-RRT*) and adaptive-extension Tube-RRT* (AETube-RRT*), while producing a shorter optimal path with a smaller cumulative turning angle. In Stage II, a convex quadratic program is formulated by considering payload translational and rotational dynamics, cable tension constraints, and collision-safety constraints, yielding a smooth, collision-free desired payload trajectory. Finally, a centralized geometric control scheme is applied to the cascaded system to validate the effectiveness and feasibility of the proposed planning framework, offering a practical solution for payload attitude maneuvering in densely cluttered environments.

</details>


### [158] [Dual Pose-Graph Semantic Localization for Vision-Based Autonomous Drone Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15168)
*David Perez-Saura,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Alvaro J. Gaona,Pascual Campoy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种双姿态图架构，用于在自主无人机竞速中实现鲁棒的实时定位，融合了里程计与语义检测信息，在TII-RATM数据集和实际比赛中显著减少了定位漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉SLAM系统在高速、剧烈机动和资源受限条件下难以应对运动模糊和特征不稳定问题，且未充分利用赛道环境的结构化特性。

Method: 设计了一种双图架构：临时图积累关键帧间的多次门框观测并优化为单个精炼约束，再加入主图；系统融合单目视觉惯性里程计与门检测结果，保持实时性能的同时提升精度。

Result: 在TII-RATM数据集上相比单独VIO将ATE减少56%~74%，消融实验显示双图比单图在相同计算成本下精度提高10%~12%，实际飞行中每圈减少漂移最多达4.2米。

Conclusion: 该双图架构能有效融合高频观测信息并维持实时性能，适用于资源受限下的高速无人机竞速场景，显著提升了定位鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: Autonomous drone racing demands robust real-time localization under extreme conditions: high-speed flight, aggressive maneuvers, and payload-constrained platforms that often rely on a single camera for perception. Existing visual SLAM systems, while effective in general scenarios, struggle with motion blur and feature instability inherent to racing dynamics, and do not exploit the structured nature of racing environments. In this work, we present a dual pose-graph architecture that fuses odometry with semantic detections for robust localization. A temporary graph accumulates multiple gate observations between keyframes and optimizes them into a single refined constraint per landmark, which is then promoted to a persistent main graph. This design preserves the information richness of frequent detections while preventing graph growth from degrading real-time performance. The system is designed to be sensor-agnostic, although in this work we validate it using monocular visual-inertial odometry and visual gate detections. Experimental evaluation on the TII-RATM dataset shows a 56% to 74% reduction in ATE compared to standalone VIO, while an ablation study confirms that the dual-graph architecture achieves 10% to 12% higher accuracy than a single-graph baseline at identical computational cost. Deployment in the A2RL competition demonstrated that the system performs real-time onboard localization during flight, reducing the drift of the odometry baseline by up to 4.2 m per lap.

</details>


### [159] [Benchmarking Classical Coverage Path Planning Heuristics on Irregular Hexagonal Grids for Maritime Coverage Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15202)
*Carlos S. Sepúlveda,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对不规则六边形网格的单车辆覆盖路径规划启发式算法的可重复基准测试，包含10,000个哈密尔顿可行实例和17种启发式方法，并揭示了实现细节（如残差度定义）对稀疏几何图上性能的重大影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典覆盖路径规划方法通常在小规模或矩形网格上进行比较，缺乏在更贴近实际海洋应用的不规则六边形网格上的系统性、可重复评估。

Method: 构建了一个基于合成但具有海洋应用背景的兴趣区域的不规则六边形图基准，包含10,000个哈密尔顿可行实例和17种来自七大家族的启发式算法，并采用统一评估协议（涵盖哈密尔顿成功、全覆盖成功、重访次数、路径长度等指标）。

Result: 实验结果显示，具有显式最短路径重连的启发式算法能可靠解决松弛覆盖任务，但几乎从不产生零重访路径；基于Warnsdorff规则并结合索引式平局打破与终端包含残差度策略的变体表现最佳，达到79.0%的哈密尔顿成功率；研究发现终点保留机制下的残差度定义是关键设计因素。

Conclusion: 该基准为启发式算法分析提供了受控测试平台，强调了在稀疏几何图中未充分报告的实现细节对性能的重要影响，但并非主张在舰队规模上的运行最优性。

Abstract: Coverage path planning on irregular hexagonal grids is relevant to maritime surveillance, search and rescue and environmental monitoring, yet classical methods are often compared on small ad hoc examples or on rectangular grids. This paper presents a reproducible benchmark of deterministic single-vehicle coverage path planning heuristics on irregular hexagonal graphs derived from synthetic but maritime-motivated areas of interest. The benchmark contains 10,000 Hamiltonian-feasible instances spanning compact, elongated, and irregular morphologies, 17 heuristics from seven families, and a common evaluation protocol covering Hamiltonian success, complete-coverage success, revisits, path length, heading changes, and CPU latency. Across the released dataset, heuristics with explicit shortest-path reconnection solve the relaxed coverage task reliably but almost never produce zero-revisit tours. Exact Depth-First Search confirms that every released instance is Hamiltonian-feasible. The strongest classical Hamiltonian baseline is a Warnsdorff variant that uses an index-based tie-break together with a terminal-inclusive residual-degree policy, reaching 79.0% Hamiltonian success. The dominant design choice is not tie-breaking alone, but how the residual degree is defined when the endpoint is reserved until the final move. This shows that underreported implementation details can materially affect performance on sparse geometric graphs with bottlenecks. The benchmark is intended as a controlled testbed for heuristic analysis rather than as a claim of operational optimality at fleet scale.

</details>


### [160] [A Hierarchical Spatiotemporal Action Tokenizer for In-Context Imitation Learning in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15215)
*Fawad Javed Fateh,Ali Shah Ali,Murad Popattia,Usman Nizamani,Andrey Konin,M. Zeeshan Zia,Quoc-Huy Tran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的分层时空动作分词器HiST-AT，用于上下文中的模仿学习，通过利用空间和时间线索进行多级聚类，并恢复输入动作及其时间戳，在多个仿真和真实机器人操作基准上实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升在上下文模仿学习中对复杂动作序列的建模能力，需要一种能够同时利用空间和时间信息的有效动作表示方法。

Method: 提出了一种包含两级向量量化的分层方法：低层级将输入动作分配到细粒度子簇，高层级进一步将这些子簇映射到更大簇；并扩展为分层时空动作分词器（HiST-AT），通过多级聚类同时重建动作和对应时间戳。

Result: 该方法优于非分层版本，并在多个模拟和真实机器人操作任务基准上取得了当前最优的性能表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的HiST-AT在上下文模仿学习中有效整合了时空信息，显著提升了动作建模与学习性能，具有较强的实用性与先进性。

Abstract: We present a novel hierarchical spatiotemporal action tokenizer for in-context imitation learning. We first propose a hierarchical approach, which consists of two successive levels of vector quantization. In particular, the lower level assigns input actions to fine-grained subclusters, while the higher level further maps fine-grained subclusters to clusters. Our hierarchical approach outperforms the non-hierarchical counterpart, while mainly exploiting spatial information by reconstructing input actions. Furthermore, we extend our approach by utilizing both spatial and temporal cues, forming a hierarchical spatiotemporal action tokenizer, namely HiST-AT. Specifically, our hierarchical spatiotemporal approach conducts multi-level clustering, while simultaneously recovering input actions and their associated timestamps. Finally, extensive evaluations on multiple simulation and real robotic manipulation benchmarks show that our approach establishes a new state-of-the-art performance in in-context imitation learning.

</details>


### [161] [Vision-Based Safe Human-Robot Collaboration with Uncertainty Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15221)
*Jakob Thumm,Marian Frei,Tianle Ni,Matthias Althoff,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉的人体姿态估计与运动预测框架，结合随机不确定性估计和异常检测，通过一致性预测集实现高置信度的安全人机协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在人机协作中实现可验证的安全性，需要对人体运动进行可靠且具有置信保证的预测。

Method: 结合了随机不确定性估计与OOD（分布外）检测，并引入一致性预测方法来生成具有高有效置信度的人体运动预测集合。

Result: 在真实的人体运动数据和实际的人机协作场景中验证了该框架的有效性，能够提供具有概率保证的预测结果。

Conclusion: 该框架能为安全关键型人机协作系统提供具有可证明置信度的人体运动预测，增强了系统的可靠性与安全性。

Abstract: We propose a framework for vision-based human pose estimation and motion prediction that gives conformal prediction guarantees for certifiably safe human-robot collaboration. Our framework combines aleatoric uncertainty estimation with OOD detection for high probabilistic confidence. To integrate our pipeline in certifiable safety frameworks, we propose conformal prediction sets for human motion predictions with high, valid confidence. We evaluate our pipeline on recorded human motion data and a real-world human-robot collaboration setting.

</details>


### [162] [Abstract Sim2Real through Approximate Information States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15289)
*Yunfu Deng,Yuhao Li,Josiah P. Hanna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在抽象模拟器中使用强化学习训练策略并成功转移到现实世界的问题，提出了一种利用现实世界任务数据校正抽象模拟器动态的方法，并验证了该方法在sim2sim和sim2real评估中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人部署在越来越复杂和广泛的领域，模拟器难以建模所有相关任务细节，因此需要研究如何在缺少关键任务细节的抽象模拟器中实现有效的sim2real迁移。

Method: 采用强化学习文献中的状态抽象语言来形式化问题，并引入一种利用现实世界任务数据校正抽象模拟器动态的方法。

Result: 所提出的方法能够在sim2sim和sim2real评估中实现成功的策略迁移。

Conclusion: 通过结合现实世界数据校正抽象模拟器的动态，可以在抽象模拟器中有效训练策略并成功迁移到现实世界。

Abstract: In recent years, reinforcement learning (RL) has shown remarkable success in robotics when a fast and accurate simulator is available for a given task. When using RL and simulation, more simulator realism is generally beneficial but becomes harder to obtain as robots are deployed in increasingly complex and widescale domains. In such settings, simulators will likely fail to model all relevant details of a given target task and this observation motivates the study of sim2real with simulators that leave out key task details. In this paper, we formalize and study the abstract sim2real problem: given an abstract simulator that models a target task at a coarse level of abstraction, how can we train a policy with RL in the abstract simulator and successfully transfer it to the real-world? Our first contribution is to formalize this problem using the language of state abstraction from the RL literature. This framing shows that an abstract simulator can be grounded to match the target task if the grounded abstract dynamics take the history of states into account. Based on the formalism, we then introduce a method that uses real-world task data to correct the dynamics of the abstract simulator. We then show that this method enables successful policy transfer both in sim2sim and sim2real evaluation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Fun-TSG: A Function-Driven Multivariate Time Series Generator with Variable-Level Anomaly Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14221)
*Pierre Lotte,André Péninou,Olivier Teste*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Fun-TSG是一个可定制的时间序列生成工具，用于支持多元时间序列异常检测方法的高质量评估，提供透明的生成过程和精细标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测基准数据集缺乏细粒度标注、变量间依赖关系和生成机制透明度，限制了模型的可靠评估与比较。

Method: 提出Fun-TSG，支持基于随机采样依赖结构和异常类型的自动生成功能，以及通过用户自定义方程的手动生成，提供变量级和时间戳级的异常真值标签。

Result: 能够生成多样化、可解释且可复现的基准数据集，支持对经典和现代异常检测模型进行细粒度性能分析。

Conclusion: Fun-TSG为异常检测方法的评估提供了灵活、透明且可控的生成平台，有助于推动可解释和变量特定检测模型的发展。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of anomaly detection methods in multivariate time series remains an open challenge, largely due to the limitations of existing benchmark datasets. Current resources often lack fine-grained anomaly annotations, do not provide explicit intervariable and temporal dependencies, and offer little insight into the underlying generative mechanisms. These shortcomings hinder the development and rigorous comparison of detection models, especially those targeting interpretable and variable-specific outputs. To address this gap, we introduce Fun-TSG, a fully customizable time series generator designed to support high-quality evaluation of anomaly detection systems. Our tool enables both fully automated generation, based on randomly sampled dependency structures and anomaly types, and manual generation through user-defined equations and anomaly configurations. In both cases, it provides full transparency over the data generation process, including access to ground-truth anomaly labels at the variable and timestamp levels. Fun-TSG supports the creation of diverse, interpretable, and reproducible benchmarking scenarios, enabling fine-grained performance analysis for both classical and modern anomaly detection models.

</details>


### [164] [GFT: From Imitation to Reward Fine-Tuning with Unbiased Group Advantages and Dynamic Coefficient Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14258)
*Wangjie Gan,Miao Pan,Linbo Xi,Wenqi Zhang,Jintao Chen,Jianwei Yin,Xuhong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Group Fine-Tuning (GFT) 的统一后训练框架，通过组优势学习和动态系数校正机制，解决了监督微调中的奖励稀疏性和优化不稳定问题，提升了模型性能和与强化学习的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）在训练过程中存在单路径依赖、熵崩溃和梯度爆炸等问题，难以兼顾高效知识注入与良好泛化能力。

Method: 提出了Group Fine-Tuning (GFT)，包括组优势学习（构建多样化响应组并生成归一化对比监督信号）和动态系数校正（自适应限制逆概率权重以稳定优化）。

Result: 实验表明GFT consistently 超过基于SFT的方法，并能更平滑地与后续的强化学习训练结合。

Conclusion: GFT有效缓解了SFT中的内在缺陷，提供了一个更稳定且高效的统一后训练框架。

Abstract: Large language models are typically post-trained using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), yet effectively unifying efficient knowledge injection with robust generalization remains challenging. In this work, we provide a training-dynamics analysis showing that SFT can be interpreted as a special case of policy gradient optimization with an extremely sparse implicit reward and unstable inverse-probability weighting, which together lead to single-path dependency, entropy collapse, and gradient explosion. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose Group Fine-Tuning (GFT), a unified post-training framework that addresses these intrinsic limitations through two mechanisms: Group Advantage Learning, which constructs diverse response groups and derives normalized contrastive supervision to alleviate reward sparsity, and Dynamic Coefficient Rectification, which adaptively bounds inverse-probability weights to stabilize optimization while preserving efficient knowledge injection. Experiments demonstrate that GFT consistently surpasses SFT-based methods and yields policies that integrate more smoothly with subsequent RL training.

</details>


### [165] [Seeing Through Experts Eyes A Foundational Vision Language Model Trained on Radiologists Gaze and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14316)
*Kinhei Lee,Peiyuan Jing,Zhenxuan Zhang,Yue Yang,Tao Wang,Dominic C Marshall,Yingying Fang,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GazeX是一种结合放射科医生眼动数据的视觉语言模型，通过将专家注意力模式融入预训练，提升胸部X光解读的准确性、可解释性和临床一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模视觉语言模型在胸部X光自动解读中缺乏对放射科医生实际观察和推理过程的模拟，导致诊断流程不一致、关键发现易被遗漏，限制了其临床应用价值。

Method: 提出GazeX模型，在预训练中引入放射科医生的眼动轨迹和注视模式作为行为先验，利用超过3万张眼动关键帧数据，使模型学习专家的时空注意力结构，并按临床有意义的顺序整合观察结果。

Result: 在30多万项放射学研究上验证，GazeX在报告生成、疾病定位和视觉问答任务中均表现出更高的准确性、可解释性和与专家判断的一致性，并能输出可验证的证据（如检查路径和定位区域）。

Conclusion: 通过学习专家的视觉行为，GazeX为构建更可信、可解释且诊断稳健的医学AI系统提供了可行路径，支持安全的人机协作。

Abstract: Large scale vision language models have shown promise in automating chest Xray interpretation, yet their clinical utility remains limited by a gap between model outputs and radiologist reasoning. Most systems optimize for semantic information without emulating how experts visually examine medical images, often overlooking critical findings or diverging from established diagnostic workflows. Radiologists follow structured protocols (e.g., the ABCDEF approach) that ensure all clinically relevant regions are systematically examined, reducing missed findings and supporting reliable diagnostic reasoning. We introduce GazeX, a vision language model that leverages radiologists' eye tracking data as a behavioral prior to model expert diagnostic reasoning. By incorporating gaze trajectories and fixation patterns into pretraining, GazeX learns to follow the spatial and temporal structure of radiologist attention and integrates observations in a clinically meaningful sequence. Using a curated dataset of over 30,000 gaze key frames from five radiologists, we demonstrate that GazeX produces more accurate, interpretable, and expert consistent outputs across radiology report generation, disease grounding, and visual question answering, utilizing 231,835 radiographic studies, 780,014 question answer pairs, and 1,162 image sentence pairs with bounding boxes. Unlike autonomous reporting systems, GazeX produces verifiable evidence artifacts, including inspection trajectories and finding linked localized regions, enabling efficient human verification and safe human AI collaboration. Learning through expert eyes provides a practical route toward more trustworthy, explainable, and diagnostically robust AI systems for radiology and beyond.

</details>


### [166] [Equifinality in Mixture of Experts: Routing Topology Does Not Determine Language Modeling Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14419)
*Ivan Ternovtsii,Yurii Bilak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏混合专家（MoE）模型中路由拓扑结构对语言建模质量的影响，提出了一种基于余弦相似度的几何MoE（ST-MoE），发现路由机制的复杂性并不决定最终的语言模型性能，在多种路由变体之间实现了等效的困惑度表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究MoE架构中日益复杂的路由机制是否真正影响语言模型的最终性能，尤其是路由拓扑结构是否决定模型质量。

Method: 设计一种低维空间中的余弦相似度路由机制（d_space=64），构建几何MoE（ST-MoE），并在WikiText-103和OpenWebText数据集上进行62次受控实验，比较不同路由策略（包括学习路由、哈希、随机固定、top-1等）在收敛状态下的困惑度表现，使用TOST方法进行统计等价性检验。

Result: 在76-84M参数规模下，五种余弦路由变体在困惑度上统计等价（PPL范围33.93–34.72，两两比较TOST p<0.05）；哈希、随机固定和top-1路由仅造成轻微退化（1.1–2.2 PPL）；在线性路由器多5.3倍参数的情况下，等参数余弦路由可弥补67%的差距，实际优势仅约1.2%；多跳更新具有高度共线性（cos=0.805），表明为幅度放大而非组合推理；单个可学习标量即可复现多跳效果；零样本相对范数停止机制节省25% MoE FLOPs，仅增加0.12% PPL。

Conclusion: 路由拓扑结构并不决定语言模型的渐近性能，复杂的路由机制带来的收益有限，真正的性能提升主要来自参数量差异而非拓扑设计；模型通过“收敛冗余”实现功能等效，简单的路由机制足以达到相近效果，为高效MoE设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures employ increasingly sophisticated routing mechanisms -- learned routers, multi-hop trajectories, token-dependent gating. We ask: does routing topology actually determine language modeling quality? We build a geometric MoE (ST-MoE) using cosine-similarity routing against learned centroids in a low-dimensional space ($d_{space} = 64$), requiring 80% fewer routing parameters than standard linear routers. Through 62 controlled experiments on WikiText-103 at 76--84M parameters trained to convergence (50K steps, 1.64B tokens), we find that routing topology does not determine asymptotic perplexity (PPL): five cosine-routing variants are statistically equivalent within a 1-PPL margin (Two One-Sided Tests [TOST], $p < 0.05$ for all 10 pairwise comparisons; 15 runs across 3 seeds, observed range 33.93--34.72). The finding extends to hash, random-fixed, and top-1 routing (single-seed; graceful 1.1--2.2 PPL degradation) and replicates on OpenWebText (0.03 PPL gap, 6 runs, 3 seeds each). A standard linear router with 5.3$\times$ more routing parameters reaches PPL 32.76, but iso-parameter cosine routing closes 67% of this gap -- the true mechanism advantage is $\sim$1.2%. The mechanistic explanation is convergent redundancy: multi-hop updates are collinear ($\cos(Δh_0, Δh_1) = 0.805$), implementing magnitude amplification rather than compositional reasoning; a single learnable scalar replicates multi-hop performance. As a practical payoff, zero-shot relative-norm halting saves 25% of MoE FLOPs at +0.12% PPL. Expert-level specialization and causal controllability -- which coexist with topology-level equifinality -- are explored in a companion paper.

</details>


### [167] [Demonstration of Pneuma-Seeker: Agentic System for Reifying and Fulfilling Information Needs on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14422)
*Muhammad Imam Luthfi Balaka,Raul Castro Fernandez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pneuma-Seeker是一个利用大语言模型作为透明、交互式分析协作者的系统，帮助数据分析师通过显式的、可检查的关系规范来迭代地明确和细化信息需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分析师在处理关系型数据时常常从模糊或未充分定义的问题出发，需要在探索数据的过程中不断迭代优化问题。现有工具往往将LLM视为黑箱答案引擎，缺乏透明性和可追溯性，难以支持有效的迭代分析。

Method: 提出Pneuma-Seeker系统，将用户的信息需求具体化为显式的、可检查的关系规范，并结合LLM作为交互式协作伙伴，支持对信息需求的迭代改进、有针对性的数据发现以及具备溯源能力的执行过程。

Result: 通过两个真实世界的采购案例展示了系统的有效性，表明Pneuma-Seeker能够提升分析过程的透明度与可控性，支持用户更好地理解和优化其分析流程。

Conclusion: Pneuma-Seeker通过将LLM融入结构化分析流程，实现了更透明、可追溯和可交互的迭代数据分析，优于传统将LLM作为黑箱使用的方式。

Abstract: Data analysts working with relational data often start with vague or underspecified questions and refine them iteratively as they explore the data. To support this iterative process, we demonstrate Pneuma-Seeker, a system that reifies a user's information need as explicit, inspectable relational specifications, enabling iterative refinement of the information need, targeted data discovery, and provenance-aware execution. Through two real-world procurement use cases, we show how Pneuma-Seeker leverages LLMs as transparent, interactive analytical collaborators rather than opaque answer engines.

</details>


### [168] [On Tackling Complex Tasks with Reward Machines and Signal Temporal Logics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14440)
*Ana María Gómez Ruiz,Thao Dang,Alexandre Donzé*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于强化学习的控制设计框架，通过将奖励机与信号时序逻辑（STL）结合，提升复杂任务中奖励表示的效率，并引导训练过程满足指定需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更高效地处理复杂任务中的奖励设计，并确保强化学习策略能够满足形式化的行为规范要求。

Method: 扩展奖励机（Reward Machines）的概念，引入信号时序逻辑（STL）公式用于事件生成，并结合STL在线监控算法实现该框架。

Result: 在微电网、倒立摆和高速公路三个非平凡任务的案例研究中验证了该框架的有效性，显示出其在奖励表示和行为引导方面的优势。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效提升复杂任务下强化学习的训练效率和行为合规性，具有良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose a Reinforcement Learning (RL) based control design framework for handling complex tasks. The approach extends the concept of Reward Machines (RM) with Signal Temporal Logic (STL) formulas that can be used for event generation. The use of STL allows not only a more efficient representation of rewards for complex tasks but also guiding the training process to converge towards behaviors satisfying specified requirements. We also propose an implementation of the framework that leverages the STL online monitoring algorithms. We illustrate the framework with three case studies (minigrid, cart-pole and high-way environments) with non-trivial tasks.

</details>


### [169] [Improving Human Performance with Value-Aware Interventions: A Case Study in Chess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14465)
*Saumik Narayanan,Raja Panjwani,Siddhartha Sen,Chien-Ju Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于策略-价值不一致性的AI干预方法，通过最大化人类价值函数来优化决策辅助，在单次和多次干预场景下均优于传统基于最优模型的干预方式，并在国际象棋任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助系统通常推荐模型认为最优的动作，但人类可能无法执行后续最优动作，导致整体表现下降。因此需要一种更符合人类实际策略的干预机制。

Method: 基于强化学习中的Bellman方程，利用策略与价值函数之间的不一致性识别干预时机；在马尔可夫决策过程框架下，定义单次干预的最优策略为最大化人类价值函数，对于多次干预则根据不一致性程度排序进行近似求解。

Result: 在国际象棋数据上训练人类行为模型，模拟实验显示该方法在多种设置下优于基于Stockfish引擎的干预；包含20名玩家、600局游戏的人类实验表明，该方法显著提升低中水平玩家表现，对高水平玩家效果与专家引擎相当。

Conclusion: 策略-价值不一致性是识别有效AI干预机会的有效指标，所提方法能更贴合人类决策特点，实现更高效的协作式决策支持。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly used to assist humans in sequential decision-making tasks, yet determining when and how an AI assistant should intervene remains a fundamental challenge. A potential baseline is to recommend the optimal action according to a strong model. However, such actions assume optimal follow-up actions, which human decision makers may fail to execute, potentially reducing overall performance. In this work, we propose and study value-aware interventions, motivated by a basic principle in reinforcement learning: under the Bellman equation, the optimal policy selects actions that maximize the immediate reward plus the value function. When a decision maker follows a suboptimal policy, this policy-value consistency no longer holds, creating discrepancies between the actions taken by the policy and those that maximize the immediate reward plus the value of the next state. We show that these policy-value inconsistencies naturally identify opportunities for intervention. We formalize this problem in a Markov decision process where an AI assistant may override human actions under an intervention budget. In the single-intervention regime, we show that the optimal strategy is to recommend the action that maximizes the human value function. For settings with multiple interventions, we propose a tractable approximation that prioritizes interventions based on the magnitude of the policy-value discrepancy. We evaluate these ideas in the domain of chess by learning models of humans from large-scale gameplay data. In simulation, our approach consistently outperforms interventions based on the strongest chess engine (Stockfish) in a wide range of settings. A within-subject human study with 20 players and 600 games further shows that our interventions significantly improve performance for low- and mid-skill players while matching expert-engine interventions for high-skill players.

</details>


### [170] [Response-Aware User Memory Selection for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14473)
*Jillian Fisher,Jennifer Neville,Chan Young Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于信息论的用户记忆选择方法RUMS，通过最大化记忆子集与模型输出之间的互信息来优化个性化大语言模型的记忆选择，相比现有方法在响应质量、计算效率和可扩展性方面均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要基于语义相似性选择用户记忆，忽略了记忆项对模型响应分布的实际影响，缺乏理论依据且与人类选择行为不一致。

Method: 提出Response-Utility优化的记忆选择（RUMS）方法，利用互信息衡量记忆子集对模型输出不确定性的减少程度，从而选择最具信息增益的记忆项。

Result: RUMS在响应质量上优于现有方法，计算成本最多降低95%，并支持高达400倍规模的模型应用。

Conclusion: 基于信息论的RUMS方法为用户记忆选择提供了更合理的原则，显著提升了个性化大语言模型的效果与效率。

Abstract: A common approach to personalization in large language models (LLMs) is to incorporate a subset of the user memory into the prompt at inference time to guide the model's generation. Existing methods select these subsets primarily using similarity between user memory items and input queries, ignoring how features actually affect the model's response distribution. We propose Response-Utility optimization for Memory Selection (RUMS), a novel method that selects user memory items by measuring the mutual information between a subset of memory and the model's outputs, identifying items that reduce response uncertainty and sharpen predictions beyond semantic similarity. We demonstrate that this information-theoretic foundation enables more principled user memory selection that aligns more closely with human selection compared to state-of-the-art methods, and models $400\times$ larger. Additionally, we show that memory items selected using RUMS result in better response quality compared to existing approaches, while having up to $95\%$ reduction in computational cost.

</details>


### [171] [Pushing the Limits of On-Device Streaming ASR: A Compact, High-Accuracy English Model for Low-Latency Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14493)
*Nenad Banfic,David Fan,Kunal Vaishnavi,Sam Kemp,Sunghoon Choi,Rui Ren,Sayan Shaw,Meng Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对多种先进的自动语音识别（ASR）架构进行了系统性实证研究，评估了其在边缘设备上进行流式推理时的准确性、延迟和内存占用表现。研究表明，NVIDIA Nemotron Speech Streaming 经过优化后是资源受限硬件上实时英文流式识别的最佳候选方案。通过在 ONNX Runtime 中重构推理流程并应用量化与算子融合等优化手段，模型体积显著减小，同时保持接近全精度模型的识别准确率，实现了新的效率与质量平衡点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无GPU加速的边缘设备上部署高质量ASR系统，需在准确性、延迟和内存之间取得平衡，但不同架构和推理模式的综合性能尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 对包括编码器-解码器、转导器和基于大语言模型的ASR架构进行跨批处理、分块和流式模式的全面基准测试，并在ONNX Runtime中重实现Nemotron的流式推理管道，结合k-quant、混合精度和算子融合等量化策略进行优化。

Result: Nemotron Speech Streaming经优化后模型大小从2.47GB降至0.67GB，词错误率（WER）保持在全精度基线1%以内；int4 k-quant版本在八个标准基准上平均流式WER为8.20%，CPU上算法延迟为0.56秒，优于实时速度。

Conclusion: NVIDIA Nemotron Speech Streaming结合后训练量化与推理优化，可在资源受限设备上实现高效、低延迟、高精度的流式ASR，确立了新的质量-效率帕累托最优点。

Abstract: Deploying high-quality automatic speech recognition (ASR) on edge devices requires models that jointly optimize accuracy, latency, and memory footprint while operating entirely on CPU without GPU acceleration. We conduct a systematic empirical study of state-of-the-art ASR architectures, encompassing encoder-decoder, transducer, and LLM-based paradigms, evaluated across batch, chunked, and streaming inference modes. Through a comprehensive benchmark of over 50 configurations spanning OpenAI Whisper, NVIDIA Nemotron, Parakeet TDT, Canary, Conformer Transducer, and Qwen3-ASR, we identify NVIDIA's Nemotron Speech Streaming as the strongest candidate for real-time English streaming on resource-constrained hardware. We then re-implement the complete streaming inference pipeline in ONNX Runtime and conduct a controlled evaluation of multiple post-training quantization strategies, including importance-weighted k-quant, mixed-precision schemes, and round-to-nearest quantization, combined with graph-level operator fusion. These optimizations reduce the model from 2.47 GB to as little as 0.67 GB while maintaining word error rate (WER) within 1% absolute of the full-precision PyTorch baseline. Our recommended configuration, the int4 k-quant variant, achieves 8.20% average streaming WER across eight standard benchmarks, running comfortably faster than real-time on CPU with 0.56 s algorithmic latency, establishing a new quality-efficiency Pareto point for on-device streaming ASR.

</details>


### [172] [Improving Machine Learning Performance with Synthetic Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14498)
*Mel Sohm,Charles Dezons,Sami Sellami,Oscar Ninou,Axel Pincon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了金融机器学习中合成数据增强的统计作用，提出了一个形式化框架来分析其偏差-方差权衡，并通过控制实验和真实数据验证了合成数据仅在方差主导的情境下有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据被广泛用于缓解金融数据稀缺问题，但其对模型性能的影响机制尚不明确，尤其是其如何改变训练分布并影响泛化性能。

Method: 将合成数据增强形式化为对有效训练分布的修改，提出一种规模匹配的零假设增强方法和基于分块置换的非参数检验，以分离信息增益与样本量效应，在马尔可夫切换环境和真实金融数据上进行评估。

Result: 实验表明，当任务处于方差主导状态（如波动率预测）时，合成数据可提升性能；但在偏差主导场景（如接近有效的方向预测）中则会损害性能。稀有状态增强可能改善特定指标但与无条件推断冲突。

Conclusion: 合成数据是否提升金融机器学习性能取决于任务的偏差-方差结构，盲目使用可能导致分布失真，需根据任务特性谨慎设计增强策略。

Abstract: Synthetic augmentation is increasingly used to mitigate data scarcity in financial machine learning, yet its statistical role remains poorly understood. We formalize synthetic augmentation as a modification of the effective training distribution and show that it induces a structural bias--variance trade-off: while additional samples may reduce estimation error, they may also shift the population objective whenever the synthetic distribution deviates from regions relevant under evaluation. To isolate informational gains from mechanical sample-size effects, we introduce a size-matched null augmentation and a finite-sample, non-parametric block permutation test that remains valid under weak temporal dependence.
  We evaluate this framework in both controlled Markov-switching environments and real financial datasets, including high-frequency option trade data and a daily equity panel. Across generators spanning bootstrap, copula-based models, variational autoencoders, diffusion models, and TimeGAN, we vary augmentation ratio, model capacity, task type, regime rarity, and signal-to-noise. We show that synthetic augmentation is beneficial only in variance-dominant regimes, such as persistent volatility forecasting-while it deteriorates performance in bias-dominant settings, including near-efficient directional prediction. Rare-regime targeting can improve domain-specific metrics but may conflict with unconditional permutation inference. Our results provide a structural perspective on when synthetic data improves financial learning performance and when it induces persistent distributional distortion.

</details>


### [173] [Geometric Metrics for MoE Specialization: From Fisher Information to Early Failure Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14500)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息几何的框架，用于严格刻画Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）模型中的专家专业化动态，引入了两个具有理论基础的新度量指标——Fisher专业化指数（FSI）和Fisher异质性评分（FHS），在多个任务和规模上验证了其有效性，并实现了早期训练失败预测与高效干预恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE专家专业化度量（如余弦相似性、路由熵）缺乏理论基础，且在不同参数化下结论不一致，因此需要一种具备理论支撑、参数不变的分析框架。

Method: 利用信息几何方法，将专家路由分布建模为概率单纯形上的流形结构，并采用费雪信息度量和黎曼几何进行形式化分析，推导出专业化对应的测地线流行为，提出FSI和FHS两个新指标，并设计基于FHS的早期预警与干预机制。

Result: 证明了传统启发式度量不满足参数化不变性（定理1），建立了专业化与测地线流动之间的近似关系（定理2），并导出了具有理论阈值的训练失败预测器（定理3）；实验显示FSI与下游性能相关性达r=0.91±0.02，FHS可在训练10%时以AUC=0.89±0.03预测失败，比基于验证损失的早停节省40倍计算量且提前23%检测效率，干预协议实现87%恢复率。

Conclusion: 该信息几何框架为MoE的专业化提供了首个严谨的理论刻画，所提出的FSI和FHS指标具有良好的可解释性、预测能力和实用性，适用于广泛场景下的MoE训练监控与优化。

Abstract: Expert specialization is fundamental to Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model success, yet existing metrics (cosine similarity, routing entropy) lack theoretical grounding and yield inconsistent conclusions under reparameterization. We present an information-geometric framework providing the first rigorous characterization of MoE specialization dynamics. Our key insight is that expert routing distributions evolve on the probability simplex equipped with the Fisher information metric, enabling formal analysis via Riemannian geometry. We prove that standard heuristic metrics violate parameterization invariance (Theorem 1), establish that specialization corresponds to geodesic flow with quantified approximation bounds (Theorem 2), and derive a failure predictor with theoretical threshold justification (Theorem 3). The framework introduces two principled metrics: Fisher Specialization Index (FSI) achieving r=0.91+/-0.02 correlation with downstream performance, and Fisher Heterogeneity Score (FHS) predicting training failure at 10% completion with AUC=0.89+/-0.03 -- outperforming validation-loss-based early stopping by 23% while requiring 40x fewer compute cycles. We validate intervention protocols achieving 87% recovery rate when FHS>1 is detected. Comprehensive experiments across language modeling (WikiText-103, C4), vision MoE (ImageNet), and scaling studies (8-64 experts, 125M-2.7B parameters) validate our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [174] [Perspective on Bias in Biomedical AI: Preventing Downstream Healthcare Disparities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14514)
*Michal Rosen-Zvi,Yoav Kan-Tor,Michael Danziger,Agata Ferretti,Javier Aula-Blasco,Julia Falcao,Ron Shamir,Mordechai Muszkat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出，生物医学研究中的数据收集和研究优先级存在早期偏见，特别是在基因组学数据中缺乏种族和族裔信息报告，导致欧洲血统数据在训练模型中占主导地位。这种偏见可能通过基础模型的广泛应用被放大，加剧健康不平等。作者呼吁遵循来源性、开放性和评估透明性三项原则以提升生物医学AI的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示在分子层面的研究数据收集中存在的系统性偏见，尤其是对少数族裔群体的代表性不足，可能导致后续临床应用中的健康差异。

Method: 对2015至2024年间4719篇PubMed索引的组学出版物进行自动化分析，并评估常用大型数据集（如CellxGene和GEO）中的种群多样性偏差。

Result: 发现仅有少数研究报告了祖先或族裔信息，且现有大型数据集中欧洲血统数据占据主导地位，显示出显著的人群偏见。

Conclusion: 为防止偏见在生物医学AI中被固化和放大，需在整个科研社区推行‘溯源、开放、评估透明’三大原则，以促进更公平和稳健的创新。

Abstract: Healthcare disparities persist across socioeconomic boundaries, often attributed to unequal access to screening, diagnostics, and therapeutics. However, this perspective highlights that critical biases can emerge much earlier, during data collection and research prioritization, long before clinical implementation in cases where the focus of the studies and the data that is collected is at the molecular level. A vast number of studies focus on collecting omics data but the demographic information associated with these datasets is often not reported in the studies, and when it is reported, it shows big biases. An automated analysis of 4719 PubMed-indexed omics publications from 2015 to 2024 reveals that only a small fraction report ancestry or ethnicity information, with ancestry reporting improving slightly. Analysis of large-scale datasets commonly used for model training, such as CellxGene and GEO, reveals substantial population bias where European-ancestry data dominates. As biomedical foundation models become central to biomedical discovery with a paradigm in which base models are pretrained on large datasets and reusing them time and again for many different downstream tasks, they risk perpetuating or amplifying these early-stage biases, leading to cascading inequities that regulatory interventions cannot fully reverse. We propose a community-wide focus on three foundational principles: Provenance, Openness, and Evaluation Transparency to improve equity and robustness in biomedical AI. This approach aims to foster biomedical innovation that more effectively serves underserved populations and improves health outcomes.

</details>


### [175] [Mind DeepResearch Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14518)
*MindDR Team,Li Auto Inc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindDR是一个高效的多智能体深度研究框架，通过约30B参数模型和四阶段训练流程，在多个基准测试中达到领先性能，并已部署为理想汽车的在线产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一个高效、可扩展的多智能体深度研究系统，能够在较小模型规模下实现与大模型相媲美的性能，降低计算成本并提升实际应用可行性。

Method: 提出三智能体架构（规划、深度搜索、报告）和四阶段专用训练流程：SFT冷启动、Search-RL、Report-RL和偏好对齐，并构建真实中文查询评测集MindDR Bench。

Result: 在BrowseComp-ZH、BrowseComp、WideSearch、xbench-DS和DeepResearch Bench等多个基准上表现优异，分别取得45.7%、42.8%、46.5%、75.0%和52.5的成绩，在MindDR Bench上达到51.8的SOTA得分，并已应用于理想汽车的产品中。

Conclusion: MindDR证明了通过精心设计的多智能体协作架构和分阶段训练策略，小规模模型也能实现强大的深度研究能力，具备良好的实用性和推广价值。

Abstract: We present \textbf{Mind DeepResearch (MindDR)}, an efficient multi-agent deep research framework that achieves leading performance with only \textasciitilde30B-parameter models through a meticulously designed data synthesis and multi-stage training pipeline. The core innovation of MindDR lies in a collaborative three-agent architecture (Planning Agent, DeepSearch Agent, and Report Agent) and a four-stage agent-specialized training pipeline comprising SFT cold-start, Search-RL, Report-RL and preference alignment. With this regime, MindDR demonstrates competitive performance even with \textasciitilde30B-scale models. Specifically, MindDR achieves 45.7\% on BrowseComp-ZH, 42.8\% on BrowseComp, 46.5\% on WideSearch, 75.0\% on xbench-DS, and 52.5 on DeepResearch Bench, outperforming comparable-scale open-source agent systems and rivaling larger-scale models. MindDR has been deployed as an online product in Li Auto. Furthermore, we introduce \textbf{MindDR Bench}, a curated benchmark of 500 real-world Chinese queries from our internal product user interactions, evaluated through a comprehensive multi-dimensional rubric system rather than relying on a single RACE metric. On MindDR Bench, MindDR achieves a state-of-the-art score of 51.8.

</details>


### [176] [Quantifying Cross-Query Contradictions in Multi-Query LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14525)
*Rohit Kumar Salla,Ramya Manasa Amancherla,Manoj Saravanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种解决大语言模型在多查询推理中逻辑不一致问题的方法，通过构建全局可满足的信念状态，显著减少了跨查询矛盾，同时保持了单个查询的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理多个相关查询时经常产生相互矛盾的答案，缺乏全局一致性，影响推理的可靠性。

Method: 引入了一个包含390个多查询推理实例的基准测试，并提出集合级指标（如案例可满足率、矛盾密度和修订成本）；采用求解器增强方法提取承诺、验证全局可满足性并进行反例引导修复。

Result: 在四个推理领域中，该方法将跨查询矛盾显著降低（SetCons从0.56提升至0.94），同时保持每查询准确率。

Conclusion: 维持全局一致性对实现稳健的多查询推理至关重要，所提方法有效提升了大模型在复杂推理任务中的逻辑一致性。

Abstract: Large language models frequently produce mutually inconsistent answers when reasoning over multiple related queries. We study case-file logical consistency: maintaining a globally satisfiable belief state across interdependent queries. We introduce a benchmark of 390 multi-query reasoning instances with entailment/contradiction/unknown labels and propose set-level metrics including Case Satisfiability Rate, Contradiction Density and Revision Cost. Our solver-augmented approach extracts commitments, verifies global satisfiability and performs counterexample-guided repair. Across four reasoning domains, our method substantially reduces cross-query contradictions (SetCons: 0.56 to 0.94) while preserving per-query accuracy, demonstrating that global coherence is critical for robust multi-query reasoning.

</details>


### [177] [Dissecting Failure Dynamics in Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14528)
*Wei Zhu,Jian Zhang,Lixing Yu,Kun Yue,Zhiwen Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大语言模型在推理过程中错误的来源，发现错误通常源于少数早期决策点，并提出了一种基于不确定性信号的干预框架GUARD，以提高推理的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大语言模型在推理过程中为何失败，尤其是错误如何在早期产生并影响后续推理。

Method: 通过分析模型生成的推理轨迹，识别导致错误的关键转换点，并利用token级别的熵变化作为不确定性信号，设计了GUARD框架来探测和重定向这些关键过渡状态。

Result: 实验表明，基于熵信号识别的早期错误转换点可以被有效干预，使用GUARD框架能在多个基准上提升推理准确性。

Conclusion: 推理错误常源自早期非均匀分布的关键过渡点，通过针对性地干预这些高不确定性节点可显著提升推理可靠性，为扩展推理计算的方法提供了补充路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance through extended inference-time deliberation, yet how their reasoning failures arise remains poorly understood. By analyzing model-generated reasoning trajectories, we find that errors are not uniformly distributed but often originate from a small number of early transition points, after which reasoning remains locally coherent but globally incorrect. These transitions coincide with localized spikes in token-level entropy, and alternative continuations from the same intermediate state can still lead to correct solutions. Based on these observations, we introduce GUARD, a targeted inference-time framework that probes and redirects critical transitions using uncertainty signals. Empirical evaluations across multiple benchmarks confirm that interventions guided by these failure dynamics lead to more reliable reasoning outcomes. Our findings highlight the importance of understanding when and how reasoning first deviates, complementing existing approaches that focus on scaling inference-time computation.

</details>


### [178] [MARS$^2$: Scaling Multi-Agent Tree Search via Reinforcement Learning for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14564)
*Pengfei Li,Shijie Wang,Fangyuan Li,Yikun Fu,Kaifeng Liu,Kaiyan Zhang,Dazhi Zhang,Yuqiang Li,Biqing Qi,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MARS²，一种将多智能体协作与树搜索结合的强化学习框架，用于提升代码生成等复杂推理任务的性能。通过共享搜索树结构和路径级奖励分配机制，实现了多样化探索与高效信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习在推理密集型任务中因轨迹多样性不足而性能受限，单一智能体策略先验限制了探索能力，而多智能体方法通常未与结构化搜索结合。

Method: 提出MARS²框架，将搜索树建模为可学习的多智能体交互环境，多个独立优化的智能体在共享树结构中协同生成和优化候选解，并引入基于树一致奖励塑形的路径级分组优势函数以实现有效信用分配。

Result: 在代码生成基准上的实验表明，MARS²在不同模型组合和训练设置下均能持续提升性能，验证了多智能体协作与树搜索结合的有效性。

Conclusion: MARS²通过融合多智能体协作与结构化搜索，有效提升了强化学习在复杂推理任务中的探索效率和性能上限，为代码生成等任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) paradigms have demonstrated strong performance on reasoning-intensive tasks such as code generation. However, limited trajectory diversity often leads to diminishing returns, which constrains the achievable performance ceiling. Search-enhanced RL alleviates this issue by introducing structured exploration, which remains constrained by the single-agent policy priors. Meanwhile, leveraging multiple interacting policies can acquire more diverse exploratory signals, but existing approaches are typically decoupled from structured search. We propose \textbf{MARS$^2$} (Multi-Agent Reinforced Tree-Search Scaling), a unified RL framework in which multiple independently-optimized agents collaborate within a shared tree-structured search environment. MARS$^2$ models the search tree as a learnable multi-agent interaction environment, enabling heterogeneous agents to collaboratively generate and refine candidate solutions within a shared search topology. To support effective learning, we introduce a path-level group advantage formulation based on tree-consistent reward shaping, which facilitates effective credit assignment across complex search trajectories. Experiments on code generation benchmarks show that MARS$^2$ consistently improves performance across diverse model combinations and training settings, demonstrating the effectiveness of coupling multi-agent collaboration with tree search for enhancing reinforcement learning. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/TsinghuaC3I/MARTI.

</details>


### [179] [Enhancing Mental Health Counseling Support in Bangladesh using Culturally-Grounded Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14576)
*Md Arid Hasan,Azhagu Meena SP,Aditya Khan,Abu Md Akteruzzaman Bhuiyan,Helal Uddin Ahmed,Joysree Debi,Farig Sadeque,Annie En-Shiun Lee,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了基于知识图谱（KG）和检索增强生成（RAG）的方法在提升大语言模型（LLM）心理咨询响应质量中的作用，发现临床验证的结构化KG能更有效地提高回应的情境相关性、临床适当性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在心理健康咨询中具有潜力，但其回应常缺乏文化敏感性、情境关联性和临床适当性。本文旨在解决如何系统地融入领域内经过临床验证的知识以提升咨询质量的问题。

Method: 构建了一个由多学科团队参与、手动构建并经临床验证的知识图谱，捕捉压力源、干预措施与结果之间的因果关系，并将其与检索增强生成（RAG）方法进行对比，评估多种LLM在两种设置下的表现，使用BERTScore F1、SBERT余弦相似度及五项人工评估指标。

Result: 基于知识图谱的方法在情境相关性、临床适当性和实际可用性方面均优于仅使用RAG的方法，且在自动与人工评估中表现更优。

Conclusion: 结构化的、经过专家验证的知识在弥补大语言模型于心理咨询任务中的局限性方面具有关键作用，知识图谱是提升LLM辅助咨询质量的有效途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show promise in generating supportive responses for mental health and counseling applications. However, their responses often lack cultural sensitivity, contextual grounding, and clinically appropriate guidance. This work addresses the gap of how to systematically incorporate domain-specific, clinically validated knowledge into LLMs to improve counseling quality. We utilize and compare two approaches, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and a knowledge graph (KG)-based method, designed to support para-counselors. Our KG is constructed manually and clinically validated, capturing causal relationships between stressors, interventions, and outcomes, with contributions from multidisciplinary people. We evaluated multiple LLMs in both settings using BERTScore F1 and SBERT cosine similarity, as well as human evaluation across five metrics, which is designed to directly measure the effectiveness of counseling beyond similarity at the surface level. The results show that KG-based approaches consistently improve contextual relevance, clinical appropriateness, and practical usability compared to RAG alone, demonstrating that structured, expert-validated knowledge plays a critical role in addressing LLMs limitations in counseling tasks.

</details>


### [180] [ADAPT: Benchmarking Commonsense Planning under Unspecified Affordance Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14902)
*Pei-An Chen,Yong-Ching Liang,Jia-Fong Yeh,Hung-Ting Su,Yi-Ting Chen,Min Sun,Winston Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DynAfford基准和ADAPT模块，用于提升具身智能体在动态环境中对对象可操作性的感知与适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的具身智能体方法通常直接执行指令，而忽略了对象是否真正可操作的问题，缺乏对动态环境中可操作性变化的评估能力。

Method: 提出DynAfford基准来评估智能体在动态环境中的表现，并设计了ADAPT插件式模块，结合视觉-语言模型进行显式的可操作性推理，增强现有规划器的能力。

Result: 实验表明，引入ADAPT模块显著提高了智能体在已见和未见环境中的鲁棒性和任务成功率；且经过领域适配的LoRA微调视觉-语言模型优于GPT-4o等商业大模型。

Conclusion: 通过显式可操作性推理，智能体能更好适应动态环境中的变化，提升任务执行的可靠性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Intelligent embodied agents should not simply follow instructions, as real-world environments often involve unexpected conditions and exceptions. However, existing methods usually focus on directly executing instructions, without considering whether the target objects can actually be manipulated, meaning they fail to assess available affordances. To address this limitation, we introduce DynAfford, a benchmark that evaluates embodied agents in dynamic environments where object affordances may change over time and are not specified in the instruction. DynAfford requires agents to perceive object states, infer implicit preconditions, and adapt their actions accordingly. To enable this capability, we introduce ADAPT, a plug-and-play module that augments existing planners with explicit affordance reasoning. Experiments demonstrate that incorporating ADAPT significantly improves robustness and task success across both seen and unseen environments. We also show that a domain-adapted, LoRA-finetuned vision-language model used as the affordance inference backend outperforms a commercial LLM (GPT-4o), highlighting the importance of task-aligned affordance grounding.

</details>


### [181] [Prompt Optimization Is a Coin Flip: Diagnosing When It Helps in Compound AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14585)
*Xing Zhang,Guanghui Wang,Yanwei Cui,Wei Qiu,Ziyuan Li,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提示优化在复合AI系统中表现不稳定，多数情况下与随机猜测无异，但在具有可利用输出结构的任务上可能有效；通过方差分析预测试和短时头部分析可预测优化价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究提示优化在不同任务和模型上的有效性差异原因，检验现有端到端优化工具背后的假设是否成立。

Method: 进行18000次网格评估和144次优化运行，分析个体提示优化与联合优化的影响，并引入ANOVA预测试和头部分析来诊断优化潜力。

Result: 交互效应不显著（p > 0.52），提示优化仅在任务具备可利用输出结构时有效，且模型默认未采用该结构。

Conclusion: 提示优化的成功取决于任务本身的结构性潜力而非优化方法本身，提出两阶段诊断可有效判断优化是否值得进行。

Abstract: Prompt optimization in compound AI systems is statistically indistinguishable from a coin flip: across 72 optimization runs on Claude Haiku (6 methods $\times$ 4 tasks $\times$ 3 repeats), 49% score below zero-shot; on Amazon Nova Lite, the failure rate is even higher. Yet on one task, all six methods improve over zero-shot by up to $+6.8$ points. What distinguishes success from failure? We investigate with 18,000 grid evaluations and 144 optimization runs, testing two assumptions behind end-to-end optimization tools like TextGrad and DSPy: (A) individual prompts are worth optimizing, and (B) agent prompts interact, requiring joint optimization. Interaction effects are never significant ($p > 0.52$, all $F < 1.0$), and optimization helps only when the task has exploitable output structure -- a format the model can produce but does not default to. We provide a two-stage diagnostic: an \$80 ANOVA pre-test for agent coupling, and a 10-minute headroom test that predicts whether optimization is worthwhile -- turning a coin flip into an informed decision.

</details>


### [182] [GDPR Auto-Formalization with AI Agents and Human Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14607)
*Ha Thanh Nguyen,Wachara Fungwacharakorn,Sabine Wehnert,May Myo Zin,Yuntao Kong,Jieying Xue,Michał Araszkiewicz,Randy Goebel,Ken Satoh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于大语言模型和人在回路验证框架的GDPR条款自动形式化方法，通过多智能体协作生成法律场景与规则，并结合人工审核确保准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了应对完全自动化法律形式化的局限性，特别是在处理法律细微差别和上下文敏感推理时的挑战，研究旨在构建一个可靠的人机协同形式化流程。

Method: 采用角色专门化的多智能体工作流，由大语言模型生成法律场景、形式规则和原子事实，并通过包含人工评审的独立验证模块进行代表性、逻辑性和合法性审查。

Result: 成功构建了一个高质量的GDPR自动形式化数据集，分析了成功与失败案例，发现结构化验证和有针对性的人工监督对可靠的形式化至关重要。

Conclusion: 在法律条文自动形式化中，结合结构化验证与人类专家参与的混合方法比完全自动化更可靠，尤其适用于复杂和语境依赖的法律领域。

Abstract: We study the overall process of automatic formalization of GDPR provisions using large language models, within a human-in-the-loop verification framework. Rather than aiming for full autonomy, we adopt a role-specialized workflow in which LLM-based AI components, operating in a multi-agent setting with iterative feedback, generate legal scenarios, formal rules, and atomic facts. This is coupled with independent verification modules which include human reviewers' assessment of representational, logical, and legal correctness. Using this approach, we construct a high-quality dataset to be used for GDPR auto-formalization, and analyze both successful and problematic cases. Our results show that structured verification and targeted human oversight are essential for reliable legal formalization, especially in the presence of legal nuance and context-sensitive reasoning.

</details>


### [183] [El Agente Forjador: Task-Driven Agent Generation for Quantum Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14609)
*Zijian Zhang,Aiwei Yin,Amaan Baweja,Jiaru Bai,Ignacio Gustin,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为El Agente Forjador的多智能体框架，能够自主生成、验证和复用科学计算工具，显著提升科学任务求解的准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的智能体系统依赖静态、人工设计的工具集，难以适应新领域和不断演化的科学库，限制了其在科学研究中的广泛应用。

Method: 提出一个四阶段工作流：工具分析、工具生成、任务执行和迭代评估，由通用编码智能体在多智能体框架中自主完成工具的创建与复用。

Result: 在量子化学和量子动力学等24项任务中验证，工具生成与复用模式相比基线显著提高准确性；强智能体构建的工具集可被弱智能体复用，降低API成本并提升求解质量；跨领域工具可组合解决混合任务。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的智能体可通过自身科学知识和编码能力自主构建可重用工具，推动科研智能体从预设实现向任务驱动能力范式转变。

Abstract: AI for science promises to accelerate the discovery process. The advent of large language models (LLMs) and agentic workflows enables the expediting of a growing range of scientific tasks. However, most of the current generation of agentic systems depend on static, hand-curated toolsets that hinder adaptation to new domains and evolving libraries. We present El Agente Forjador, a multi-agent framework in which universal coding agents autonomously forge, validate, and reuse computational tools through a four-stage workflow of tool analysis, tool generation, task execution, and iterative solution evaluation. Evaluated across 24 tasks spanning quantum chemistry and quantum dynamics on five coding agent setups, we compare three operating modes: zero-shot generation of tools per task, reuse of a curriculum-built toolset, and direct problem-solving with the coding agents as the baseline. We find that our tool generation and reuse framework consistently improves accuracy over the baseline. We also show that reusing a toolset built by a stronger coding agent can reduce API cost and substantially raises the solution quality for weaker coding agents. Case studies further demonstrate that tools forged for different domains can be combined to solve hybrid tasks. Taken together, these results show that LLM-based agents can use their scientific knowledge and coding capabilities to autonomously build reusable scientific tools, pointing toward a paradigm in which agent capabilities are defined by the tasks they are designed to solve rather than by explicitly engineered implementations.

</details>


### [184] [CoDaS: AI Co-Data-Scientist for Biomarker Discovery via Wearable Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14615)
*Yubin Kim,Salman Rahman,Samuel Schmidgall,Chunjong Park,A. Ali Heydari,Ahmed A. Metwally,Hong Yu,Xin Liu,Xuhai Xu,Yuzhe Yang,Maxwell A. Xu,Zhihan Zhang,Cynthia Breazeal,Tim Althoff,Petar Sirkovic,Ivor Rendulic,Annalisa Pawlosky,Nicolas Stroppa,Juraj Gottweis,Elahe Vedadi,Alan Karthikesalingam,Pushmeet Kohli,Vivek Natarajan,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Hae Won Park,Hamid Palangi,Daniel McDuff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoDaS是一个多智能体系统，通过迭代过程从可穿戴设备数据中系统性地发现和验证数字生物标志物，在心理健康和代谢疾病领域识别出多个候选生物标志物，并在独立队列中验证其关联性，提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将可穿戴设备产生的连续生理信号转化为具有临床意义的数字生物标志物，以支持数字健康领域的科学发现。

Method: 提出CoDaS（AI Co-Data-Scientist），一种结合假设生成、统计分析、对抗验证和文献驱动推理的多智能体系统，辅以人工监督，对大规模可穿戴数据进行迭代分析。

Result: 在三个共9,279名参与者的队列中，CoDaS识别出41个心理健康和25个代谢相关的候选数字生物标志物；在两个抑郁症独立队列中发现与昼夜节律不稳定性相关的特征，在代谢队列中恢复了已知临床指标（如AST/ALT）并构建心血管适能指数，加入CoDaS特征后预测模型性能略有提升（ΔR²分别为0.040和0.021）。

Conclusion: CoDaS能够实现从大规模可穿戴数据中系统化、可追溯的生物标志物假设生成与优先级排序，推动数字健康研究的科学发现。

Abstract: Scientific discovery in digital health requires converting continuous physiological signals from wearable devices into clinically actionable biomarkers. We introduce CoDaS (AI Co-Data-Scientist), a multi-agent system that structures biomarker discovery as an iterative process combining hypothesis generation, statistical analysis, adversarial validation, and literature-grounded reasoning with human oversight using large-scale wearable datasets. Across three cohorts totaling 9,279 participant-observations, CoDaS identified 41 candidate digital biomarkers for mental health and 25 for metabolic outcomes, each subjected to an internal validation battery spanning replication, stability, robustness, and discriminative power. Across two independent depression cohorts, CoDaS surfaced circadian instability-related features in both datasets, reflected in sleep duration variability (DWB, ρ= 0.252, p < 0.001) and sleep onset variability (GLOBEM, ρ= 0.126, p < 0.001). In a metabolic cohort, CoDaS derived a cardiovascular fitness index (steps/resting heart rate; ρ= -0.374, p < 0.001), and recovered established clinical associations, including the hepatic function ratio (AST/ALT; ρ= -0.375, p < 0.001), a known correlate of insulin resistance. Incorporating CoDaS-derived features alongside demographic variables led to modest but consistent improvements in predictive performance, with cross-validated ΔR^2 increases of 0.040 for depression and 0.021 for insulin resistance. These findings suggest that CoDaS enables systematic and traceable hypothesis generation and prioritization for biomarker discovery from large-scale wearable data.

</details>


### [185] [A Parallel Approach to Counting Exact Covers Based on Decomposability Property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14627)
*Liangda Fang,Yaohui Luo,Delong Li,Xuanxiang Huang,Quanlong Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的基于decision-ZDNNF的并行算法DXD，并通过动态更新连通分量进行优化，在精确覆盖问题上超越了现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确覆盖问题是AI领域中的经典NP难问题，现有基于ZBDD的方法在表达能力上存在局限，需要更简洁高效的表示与求解方法。

Method: 提出了零抑制型决策可分解否定范式（decision-ZDNNF），设计了构建该结构的并行算法DXD，并引入动态更新连通分量的优化策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，改进后的DXD算法在性能上优于所有现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: decision-ZDNNF比ZBDD更具表达优势，所提出的DXD算法为精确覆盖问题提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The exact cover problem is a classical NP-hard problem with broad applications in the area of AI. Algorithm DXZ is a method to count exact covers representing by zero-suppressed binary decision diagrams (ZBDDs). In this paper, we propose a zero-suppressed variant of decision decomposable negation normal form (in short, decision-ZDNNF), which is strictly more succinct than ZBDDs. We then design a novel parallel algorithm, namely DXD, which constructs a decision-ZDNNF representing the set of all exact covers. Furthermore, we improve DXD by dynamically updating connected components. The experimental results demonstrate that the improved DXD algorithm outperforms all of state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [186] [Learning to Draw ASCII Improves Spatial Reasoning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14641)
*Shiyuan Huang,Li Liu,Jincheng He,Leilani H. Gilpin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Text2Space数据集，通过训练大语言模型构建文本到ASCII网格布局的映射，提升其空间推理能力，发现学习绘制布局能显著增强模型对空间关系的理解，并在多个基准上实现泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型是否可以通过学习构建显式视觉布局来获得真正的空间理解能力，类似于人类通过绘图理清空间关系。

Method: 构建Text2Space数据集，包含自然语言描述、真实ASCII网格布局及空间问答对；训练模型完成从文本到ASCII布局的生成（Text→ASCII），并分离布局构建与推理过程进行评估。

Result: 发现大语言模型存在“读写不对称”现象：能较好理解ASCII布局但难以从文本生成正确布局；训练布局构造能力可显著提升其纯文本空间推理表现，且效果可迁移到外部基准。

Conclusion: 学习构建显式空间布局能够有效增强大语言模型的空间理解能力，这种通过‘绘图’获得的结构化训练具有跨格式的泛化性。

Abstract: When faced with complex spatial problems, humans naturally sketch layouts to organize their thinking, and the act of drawing further sharpens their understanding. In this work, we ask whether a similar principle holds for Large Language Models (LLMs): can learning to construct explicit visual layouts from spatial descriptions instill genuine spatial understanding? We introduce Text2Space, a dataset that pairs natural language descriptions with ground-truth ASCII grid layouts and spatial QA pairs, enabling us to separate failures in constructing spatial representations from failures in reasoning over them. We adopt ASCII because it is human-readable, operates entirely within the token space of language models, and encodes spatial relations in a structurally verifiable form. Our evaluation reveals a pronounced "Read-Write Asymmetry": LLMs interpret ASCII representations effectively but struggle to produce them from text, and these construction errors propagate to incorrect answers downstream. To address this limitation, we train models on layout construction (Text$\rightarrow$ASCII) and find that it significantly improves spatial reasoning from text alone, even without producing any ASCII at inference time. Combining construction with comprehension training further amplifies these gains. Crucially, these improvements transfer to three external spatial reasoning benchmarks, demonstrating that, much as sketching sharpens human spatial thinking, learning to construct explicit layouts instills spatial understanding that generalizes beyond the training format.

</details>


### [187] [Targeted Exploration via Unified Entropy Control for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14646)
*Chen Wang,Lai Wei,Yanzhi Zhang,Chenyang Shao,Zedong Dan,Weiran Huang,Ge Lan,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为UEC-RL的强化学习框架，通过统一的熵控制机制解决GRPO中熵崩溃导致策略过早收敛的问题。该方法在困难提示下增强探索，并通过稳定器防止熵失控，从而在保持训练稳定性的同时扩大搜索空间。实验表明，UEC-RL在LLM和VLM推理任务中均优于基线方法，尤其在Geometry3K上比GRPO相对提升了37.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法（如GRPO）在语言和视觉-语言模型推理中存在熵崩溃问题，导致策略多样性丧失和过早收敛；同时，现有探索方法会引入额外偏差或方差，影响训练稳定性。因此需要一种既能促进有效探索又能保持优化稳定的方法。

Method: 提出Unified Entropy Control for Reinforcement Learning（UEC-RL），包含两个核心组件：一是针对困难提示激活更多探索的机制，以发现有价值的推理路径；二是引入稳定器防止熵过度增长，确保训练过程中策略的稳定性。二者协同工作，在扩展搜索空间的同时维持稳健优化。

Result: 在多个LLM和VLM推理任务上进行实验，UEC-RL在Pass@1和Pass@$k$指标上均优于现有的强化学习基线方法。特别是在Geometry3K数据集上，相比GRPO实现了37.9%的相对性能提升，验证了其在保持收敛性的同时持续有效探索的能力。

Conclusion: UEC-RL通过统一的熵控制机制解决了GRPO中的熵崩溃问题，能够在不牺牲训练稳定性的前提下增强模型的探索能力，是推动基于强化学习的大模型推理能力扩展的关键方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in reinforcement learning (RL) have improved the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs). However, the widely used Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) consistently suffers from entropy collapse, causing the policy to converge prematurely and lose diversity. Existing exploration methods introduce additional bias or variance during exploration, making it difficult to maintain optimization stability. We propose Unified Entropy Control for Reinforcement Learning (UEC-RL), a framework that provides targeted mechanisms for exploration and stabilization. UEC-RL activates more exploration on difficult prompts to search for potential and valuable reasoning trajectories. In parallel, a stabilizer prevents entropy from growing uncontrollably, thereby keeping training stable as the model consolidates reliable behaviors. Together, these components expand the search space when needed while maintaining robust optimization throughout training. Experiments on both LLM and VLM reasoning tasks show consistent gains over RL baselines on both Pass@1 and Pass@$k$. On Geometry3K, UEC-RL achieves a 37.9\% relative improvement over GRPO, indicating that it sustains effective exploration without compromising convergence and underscoring UEC-RL as a key for scaling RL-based reasoning in large models. Our code is available at https://github.com/597358816/UEC-RL.

</details>


### [188] [Rethinking Patient Education as Multi-turn Multi-modal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14656)
*Zonghai Yao,Zhipeng Tang,Chengtao Lin,Xiong Luo,Benlu Wang,Juncheng Huang,Chin Siang Ong,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedImageEdu是一个用于多轮、基于证据的放射学患者教育的多模态基准，结合图像和文本解释以提升患者理解，并评估模型在咨询过程和多模态响应中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学多模态基准多关注静态任务，而患者教育需要结合图像与文本进行动态、有同理心的交互；当前研究大多局限于文本形式，缺乏对视觉支持的有效利用。

Method: 构建MedImageEdu基准，包含150个来自三个来源的病例，每个病例提供放射报告和图像；设计DoctorAgent与PatientAgent交互框架，DoctorAgent可根据患者问题生成基于报告和图像的绘图指令，通过专用绘图工具生成图像并返回多模态回应；从五个维度进行评估：咨询过程、安全性与范围、语言质量、绘图质量和图文响应质量。

Result: 在多个开源和闭源视觉-语言模型上的实验表明：语言流畅性常优于视觉忠实性；安全性能是各疾病类别中最弱的一环；情绪紧张的互动比低教育或低健康素养情境更具挑战性。

Conclusion: MedImageEdu为评估多模态智能体是否能真正基于证据进行教学提供了可控测试平台，推动从‘仅回答文本’向‘结合视觉证据教学’的发展。

Abstract: Most medical multimodal benchmarks focus on static tasks such as image question answering, report generation, and plain-language rewriting. Patient education is more demanding: systems must identify relevant evidence across images, show patients where to look, explain findings in accessible language, and handle confusion or distress. Yet most patient education work remains text-only, even though combined image-and-text explanations may better support understanding. We introduce MedImageEdu, a benchmark for multi-turn, evidence-grounded radiology patient education. Each case provides a radiology report with report text and case images. A DoctorAgent interacts with a PatientAgent, conditioned on a hidden profile that captures factors such as education level, health literacy, and personality. When a patient question would benefit from visual support, the DoctorAgent can issue drawing instructions grounded in the report, case images, and the current question to a benchmark-provided drawing tool. The tool returns image(s), after which the DoctorAgent produces a final multimodal response consisting of the image(s) and a grounded plain-language explanation. MedImageEdu contains 150 cases from three sources and evaluates both the consultation process and the final multimodal response along five dimensions: Consultation, Safety and Scope, Language Quality, Drawing Quality, and Image-Text Response Quality. Across representative open- and closed-source vision-language model agents, we find three consistent gaps: fluent language often outpaces faithful visual grounding, safety is the weakest dimension across disease categories, and emotionally tense interactions are harder than low education or low health literacy. MedImageEdu provides a controlled testbed for assessing whether multimodal agents can teach from evidence rather than merely answer from text.

</details>


### [189] [Acceptance Dynamics Across Cognitive Domains in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14682)
*Saif Mahmoud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于树的推测解码在不同NLP任务中的接受动态，发现任务类型比树深度更能影响接受率，且仅在开放对话任务中平均每步接受长度超过1个token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不同认知特征的任务对推测解码接受概率的影响，填补当前研究在任务类型与接受率关系上的空白。

Method: 使用TinyLlama-1.1B作为草稿模型，Llama-2-7B-Chat-GPTQ作为目标模型，在代码生成、数学推理、逻辑推理和开放对话四个NLP领域进行实验，收集99,768个推测节点，分析每域的接受率、预期接受长度、深度-接受分布及熵-接受相关性。

Result: 任务类型是比树深度更强的接受率预测因子；只有聊天领域的预期接受长度超过1.0 token/step；熵与接受率呈弱负相关（rho ∈ [-0.20, -0.15]）；聊天任务虽熵最高但接受率也最高，归因于RLHF对齐语言的词汇可预测性。

Conclusion: 任务类型显著影响推测解码效率，建议根据领域特性设计推测预算和选择草稿模型。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model (LLM) inference. It uses a small draft model to propose a tree of future tokens. A larger target model then verifies these tokens in a single batched forward pass. Despite the growing body of work on speculative methods, the degree to which the cognitive characteristics of a task affect acceptance probability remains largely unexplored. We present an empirical study of tree-based speculative decoding acceptance dynamics. Our study spans four well-established NLP benchmark domains: code generation, mathematical reasoning, logical reasoning, and open-ended chat. For this, we use TinyLlama-1.1B as the draft model against Llama-2-7B-Chat-GPTQ as the target. Over 99,768 speculative nodes collected from 200 prompts, we derive per-domain acceptance rates, expected accepted lengths, depth-acceptance profiles, and entropy-acceptance correlations. We find that task type is a stronger predictor of acceptance than tree depth. Furthermore, only the chat domain consistently yields an expected accepted length exceeding 1.0 token per step. We also show that the entropy-acceptance correlation is consistently negative but weak across all domains (rho in [-0.20, -0.15]). Counterintuitively, chat produces the highest entropy yet the highest acceptance rate. We attribute this divergence to the lexical predictability of RLHF-aligned register. These findings have direct implications for domain-aware speculation budgets and draft-model selection strategies. Index Terms--speculative decoding, large language model inference, tree attention, draft model, acceptance probability, LLM efficiency

</details>


### [190] [M2-PALE: A Framework for Explaining Multi-Agent MCTS--Minimax Hybrids via Process Mining and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14687)
*Yiyu Qian,Liyuan Zhao,Tim Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合MCTS与Minimax的搜索方法，并通过流程挖掘和大语言模型生成可解释的决策逻辑，提升了智能体的可解释性与策略深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MCTS依赖选择性树构建，容易遗漏关键动作并陷入战术陷阱，且其复杂搜索树难以理解，导致决策行为缺乏可解释性。

Method: 在多智能体MCTS的rollout阶段引入浅层全宽Minimax搜索以增强策略深度，并提出M2-PALE框架，利用流程挖掘算法（如Alpha Miner、iDHM、Inductive Miner）从执行轨迹中提取行为工作流，再由大语言模型生成人类可读的因果性和远因性解释。

Result: 在小规模跳棋环境中验证了该方法的有效性，能够生成可解释的决策过程，并为复杂战略场景中的混合智能体提供可扩展的解释基础。

Conclusion: 结合Minimax的MCTS改进了搜索质量与鲁棒性，而M2-PALE框架显著提升了智能体决策的可解释性，为未来高风险领域中可信AI的发展提供了新路径。

Abstract: Monte-Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) is a fundamental sampling-based search algorithm widely used for online planning in sequential decision-making domains. Despite its success in driving recent advances in artificial intelligence, understanding the behavior of MCTS agents remains a challenge for both developers and users. This difficulty stems from the complex search trees produced through the simulation of numerous future states and their intricate relationships. A known weakness of standard MCTS is its reliance on highly selective tree construction, which may lead to the omission of crucial moves and a vulnerability to tactical traps. To resolve this, we incorporate shallow, full-width Minimax search into the rollout phase of multi-agent MCTS to enhance strategic depth. Furthermore, to demystify the resulting decision-making logic, we introduce \textsf{M2-PALE} (MCTS--Minimax Process-Aided Linguistic Explanations). This framework employs process mining techniques, specifically the Alpha Miner, iDHM, and Inductive Miner algorithms, to extract underlying behavioral workflows from agent execution traces. These process models are then synthesized by LLMs to generate human-readable causal and distal explanations. We demonstrate the efficacy of our approach in a small-scale checkers environment, establishing a scalable foundation for interpreting hybrid agents in increasingly complex strategic domains.

</details>


### [191] [SynHAT: A Two-stage Coarse-to-Fine Diffusion Framework for Synthesizing Human Activity Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14705)
*Rongchao Xu,Lin Jiang,Dahai Yu,Ximiao Li,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SynHAT的高效粗到细人类活动轨迹（HAT）合成框架，基于新型时空去噪扩散模型，能够生成高保真、隐私保护且计算高效的合成轨迹数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于隐私问题限制了真实大规模HAT数据的获取，同时HAT具有高度不规则性和动态性，现有生成模型难以有效建模其复杂时空依赖并高效生成长期细粒度轨迹。

Method: 提出两阶段合成框架SynHAT：第一阶段使用Coarse-HADiff建模粗粒度潜在时空轨迹的全局依赖，结合新型Latent Spatio-Temporal U-Net与双Drift-Jitter分支；第二阶段通过行为模式提取、Fine-HADiff和语义对齐三步生成细粒度轨迹。

Result: 在四个城市的真实HAT数据集上实验表明，SynHAT在空间和时间指标上分别比现有最优方法提升52%和33%，并在数据保真度、实用性、隐私性、鲁棒性和可扩展性方面表现优越。

Conclusion: SynHAT有效解决了HAT生成中的复杂时空建模与计算效率难题，为隐私敏感场景下的轨迹数据应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Human activity traces (HATs) are critical for many applications, including human mobility modeling and point-of-interest (POI) recommendation. However, growing privacy concerns have severely limited access to authentic large-scale HAT datasets. Recent advances in generative AI provide new opportunities to synthesize realistic and privacy-preserving HATs for such applications. Yet two major challenges remain: (i) HATs are highly irregular and dynamic, with long and varying time intervals, making it difficult to capture their complex spatio-temporal dependencies and underlying distributions; and (ii) generative models are often computationally expensive, making long-term, fine-grained HAT synthesis inefficient. To address these challenges, we propose SynHAT, a computationally efficient coarse-to-fine HAT synthesis framework built on a novel spatio-temporal denoising diffusion model. In Stage 1, we develop Coarse-HADiff, which models the overall spatio-temporal dependencies of coarse-grained latent spatio-temporal traces. It incorporates a novel Latent Spatio-Temporal U-Net with dual Drift-Jitter branches to jointly model smooth spatial transitions and temporal variations during denoising. In Stage 2, we introduce a three-step pipeline consisting of Behavior Pattern Extraction, Fine-HADiff, which shares the same architecture as Coarse-HADiff, and Semantic Alignment to generate fine-grained latent spatio-temporal traces from the Stage 1 outputs. We extensively evaluate SynHAT in terms of data fidelity, utility, privacy, robustness, and scalability. Experiments on real-world HAT datasets from four cities across three countries show that SynHAT substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving 52% and 33% improvements on spatial and temporal metrics, respectively.

</details>


### [192] [SGA-MCTS: Decoupling Planning from Execution via Training-Free Atomic Experience Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14712)
*Xin Xie,Dongyun Xue,Wuguannan Yao,Mingxiao Feng,Wengang Zhou,Xiang Qi,Houqiang Li,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SGA-MCTS是一种将大语言模型规划转化为非参数化检索的框架，通过离线蒙特卡洛树搜索生成抽象的状态-目标-动作原子，并在线检索与重映射这些原子，实现无需微调的高效推理，兼顾推理深度与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型规划方法在推理时搜索延迟高，或监督微调泛化能力有限，难以兼顾效率与通用性。

Method: 提出SGA-MCTS框架：离线阶段利用MCTS探索解空间并提取去词汇化的状态-目标-动作（SGA）原子；在线阶段通过混合符号-语义机制检索相关SGA并重新接地为软推理提示，辅助决策。

Result: 在多个复杂基准上实验表明，该方法使冻结的开源模型无需任务特定微调即可达到最先进系统（如GPT-5）的性能水平。

Conclusion: SGA-MCTS通过将搜索成本摊销到离线阶段，实现了系统2级别的深度推理与系统1级别的快速推断，使自主规划具备可扩展性和实时可行性。

Abstract: LLM-powered systems require complex multi-step decision-making abilities to solve real-world tasks, yet current planning approaches face a trade-off between the high latency of inference-time search and the limited generalization of supervised fine-tuning. To address this limitation, we introduce \textbf{SGA-MCTS}, a framework that casts LLM planning as non-parametric retrieval. Offline, we leverage Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to explore the solution space and distill high-fidelity trajectories into State-Goal-Action (SGA) atoms. These atoms are de-lexicalized primitives that abstract concrete entities into symbolic slots, preserving reusable causal logic while discarding domain-specific noise. Online, a retrieval-augmented agent employs a hybrid symbolic-semantic mechanism to fetch relevant SGAs and re-ground them into the current context as soft reasoning hints. Empirical results on complex benchmarks demonstrate that this paradigm enables frozen, open-weights models to match the performance of SOTA systems (e.g., GPT-5) without task-specific fine-tuning. By effectively amortizing the heavy computational cost of search, SGA-MCTS achieves System 2 reasoning depth at System 1 inference speeds, rendering autonomous planning both scalable and real-time feasible.

</details>


### [193] [The Agentification of Scientific Research: A Physicist's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14718)
*Xiao-Liang Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为AI革命最重要的意义在于改变复杂信息和人类知识的传递与共享方式，而非简单的自动化。AI for Science将重塑科研协作、发现、出版和评估的结构，并推动AI从研究工具转变为科学合作者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨AI革命，尤其是大语言模型兴起对科学领域的影响，超越自动化视角，关注信息传递与知识共享的根本变革。

Method: 通过理论分析和路径推演，阐述AI如何逐步从科研工具演变为科学合作者，并讨论其对科研出版等体系的深远影响。

Result: 提出AI将深刻改变科学研究的结构，包括协作模式、发现过程、出版形式和评价机制；强调持续学习和思想多样性对AI参与原创性科学发现的重要性。

Conclusion: AI for Science的意义不仅在于提升效率，更在于重构科学活动本身的组织形式，未来需重视AI的持续学习能力与多元思想融合以实现真正的科学创新。

Abstract: This article argues that the most important significance of the AI revolution, especially the rise of large language models, lies not simply in automation, but in a fundamental change in how complex information and human know-how are carried, replicated, and shared. From this perspective, AI for Science is especially important because it may transform not only the efficiency of research, but also the structure of scientific collaboration, discovery, publishing, and evaluation. The article outlines a gradual path from AI as a research tool to AI as a scientific collaborator, and discusses how AI is likely to fundamentally reshape scientific publication.
